While red teaming strategies help mitigate specific risks, broader concerns persist regarding linguistic constraints, biases, and the sycophantic tendencies of LLMs. This chapter explores how LLMs can be used to significantly scale up and democratise deliberation, particularly in fostering inclusivity and empowering traditionally marginalised groups. Drawing on concepts from Systemic-Functional Linguistics, the chapter examines how variations across language users (for example, with respect to socio-demographic groups) and across language use (for example, with respect to communicative functions) shape participation in AI-supported deliberation. The chapter presents AI-driven deliberation studies and assesses their potential to scaffold argumentation, enhance access, and reduce the influence of exclusionary linguistic norms and biases which are embedded in prestigious registers. At the same time, the chapter cautions against both overclaiming, which leads to unrealistic expectations, and underclaiming, which risks missed opportunities for AI-assisted engagement. The chapter concludes by identifying future research directions to maximise the democratic potential of AI-assisted participation while embedding ethical safeguards to counteract the reproduction of linguistic inequalities.

</details>


### [38] [REDACT: A Systematically Controlled Multilingual Benchmark for Personal Information Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19881)
*Guneesh Vats,Anubha Agrawal,Shikha Singhal,Ajita Dash,Praison Selvaraj,Vidhan Jhawar,Ranga Prasad Chenna,Bharadwaj Y M G*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对现有PII检测基准的局限性，作者提出了涵盖多语言、多指标的REDACT基准测试，揭示了在处理高敏感度数据时，现有的规则检测器远弱于LLM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的个人身份信息（PII）检测基准语料覆盖实体类型单一、生成条件随意，且缺乏对模型失效原因（表面条件）的系统性分析。

Method: 构建了一个包含25种语言、13427条记录的受控基准测试集REDACT，使用覆盖数组采样器控制9个生成轴（领域、格式、难度、长度等），并引入元数据字段（披露状态、形式、GDPR敏感度分级）进行分层评估。

Result: 评估了5种检测器，发现聚合F1分数掩盖了架构间的失效差异：规则基础检测器在最高敏感度类别上的召回率仅为0.07，而LLM检测器在处理最高敏感度数据时展现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 传统的基于规则的检测器在高敏感度数据和非字面披露形式上表现不佳，而大模型（LLM）检测器表现更稳健；研究表明敏感度分级是当前PII检测任务中最具挑战性的维度。

Abstract: Benchmark infrastructure for personally identifiable information (PII) detection remains limited: existing corpora cover few entity types, use ad hoc generation conditions, and do not show which surface conditions cause detector failures. We present REDACT, a systematically controlled multilingual PII benchmark with 13,427 records, 324,078 entity annotations, 51 entity types, 4,127 surface-form patterns, and 25 languages across 9 scripts. A strength-2 covering-array sampler controls nine generation axes: domain, format, difficulty, length, density, code-switching, language, adjacency, and co-occurrence. Three entity-level metadata fields (disclosure status, disclosure form, and a GDPR-aligned sensitivity tier) enable stratified evaluation beyond aggregate or per-type F1. From the full benchmark, we evaluate five detectors (Presidio, GLiNER, the OpenAI Privacy Filter, GPT-4.1, and Claude Sonnet 4.6) on a locked, language-stratified sample of 1,000 records. Aggregate F1 masks an architecture-dependent failure structure: the rule-based detector performs poorly on the highest-stakes data, including HIGH-sensitivity categories (recall 0.07) and non-verbatim disclosure forms, while the LLM detectors remain more robust, with the HIGH tier as their strongest sensitivity slice. A three-model reference-free LLM-as-judge assessment corroborates that sensitivity-tier assignment is the task's hardest axis. We release the benchmark, schema, prompts, and stratified evaluation harness.

</details>


### [39] [Light-weight Pronunciation Assessment via Discrete Speech Token Surprisal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19910)
*Syeda Faiza Ahmed Sara,Shammur Absar Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种仅依赖原生语音训练的轻量级自动化发音评估框架，通过令牌惊喜度和文本引导的对齐机制，无需大量人类标注数据即可实现接近监督学习的评估性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动发音评估（APE）的高度依赖于昂贵的带标签学习者误差数据或专门的非母语语料库，数据获取成本高且难以规模化。

Method: 1. 使用SSL编码器和K-means码本将语音离散化；2. 基于原生语音训练令牌语言模型，通过计算“惊喜度”（Surprisal）来检测音素搭配偏差；3. 引入Transcript-guided Text2DUnit-DTW模块，将参考文本转化为原生令牌序列并与声学令牌对齐，提取误差敏感特征；4. 使用简单回归将惊喜度和对齐特征进行融合。

Result: 在SpeechOcean762数据集上，PCC指标从0.60提升至0.66，接近监督学习基准。在L2-ARCTIC数据集上的交叉评估也验证了该方法的有效性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了利用原生语音资源进行无监督或轻度校准的框架在自动发音评估（APE）中是有效的，其性能接近监督学习基准，且具备更好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Training automated pronunciation assessment often relies on labeled learner errors or non-native corpora that are costly to collect. We propose a lightweight framework trained only on native speech resources, operating unsupervised or lightly calibrated with a small set of scored utterances. At inference, learner speech is discretized with an SSL encoder and a K-means codebook. A token language model trained on native sequences computes surprisal where higher surprisal indicates phonotactic deviation. We add a transcript-guided Text2DUnit--DTW module that predicts native token sequences from reference text and aligns them to acoustic tokens to derive error-sensitive features. Surprisal and alignment features are fused via simple regression. On SpeechOcean762, PCC improves from 0.60 to 0.66 with transcript guidance, near supervised baselines. Cross-dataset evaluation on L2-ARCTIC shows consistent gains.

</details>


### [40] [GEMS: Geometric Constraints Enable Multi-Semantic Superposition in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19946)
*Yu Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大模型激活引导中多方向叠加导致的性能崩溃问题，本文提出了 GEMS 框架，通过引入范数保持和实时正交化几何约束，实现了在不微调的情况下对模型行为进行稳健的多维度并行控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的激活引导（Activation Steering）方法仅支持单方向注入，在无约束叠加多个语义方向时会导致模型输出坍塌（Collapse），缺乏通用的多方向干预框架。

Method: 提出了 GEMS（Geometric Steering）方法，该方法包含两项核心几何约束：1. 针对分布偏移的范数保持加权叠加与目标注意力路径注入；2. 针对方向干扰的实时正交化处理。

Result: 在 GSM8K 测试中，GEMS 在注入三个干扰方向后准确率保持在 98%（基准 92%，未约束则跌至 4%）；在 Wikitext-2 上仅造成 2.2% 的困惑度（PPL）增加。实验证实该方法在不同参数规模（3B-31B）下具有跨架构的通用性。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的 GEMS 方法通过正交化和范数约束，成功解决了多语义方向叠加导致的模型性能崩溃问题，验证了训练期间无需微调即可实现稳健的多维度模型控制。

Abstract: Activation steering controls model behavior by modifying intermediate hidden states at inference time without retraining. Existing methods handle only single-direction injection; when multiple semantic directions are superposed without constraints, the model collapses. We show that this collapse decomposes into two independently acting sources: distributional deviation, where additive perturbations accumulate in norm across layers and drive activations outside the training distribution, and directional interference, where non-orthogonal semantic vectors mutually dampen when superposed. These two sources define the design constraints that any training-free multi-directional intervention must address. As one instantiation of these principles, we propose GEMS, a training-free method that maps each source to a corresponding geometric constraint: norm-preserving weighted superposition and targeted attention-pathway injection for distributional deviation, and real-time orthogonalization for directional interference. On GSM8K, injecting three concurrent non-mathematical directions preserves accuracy at 98% (baseline 92%), while unconstrained addition collapses to 4%; on Wikitext-2, the same injection incurs only 2.2% PPL increase. Component ablation isolates the causal role of each constraint, and layer-level probes confirm that orthogonalized signals survive the FFN pathway and reach the output distribution with semantic specificity. Qualitative steering effects transfer across architectures from 3B to 31B.

</details>


### [41] [Source-Grounded Data Generation for Text-to-JSON Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20072)
*Sunghee Ahn,Guijin Son,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为解决文本到JSON提取任务中高质量训练数据稀缺的问题，研究提出了STAGE方法，通过电子表格源数据验证来合成更可靠的训练样本，大幅提升了模型的提取精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统行业中存在大量非结构化文档，将其转换为结构化JSON数据以供自动化系统使用面临训练数据构建困难、不可靠及难以扩展的挑战。

Method: 提出了STAGE（Spreadsheet-grounded Text-to-JSON Artifact GEneration）流程，利用LLM合成报告与JSON架构，并使用底层电子表格作为真实值（ground-truth）对生成的训练数据进行验证。

Result: 在STAGE-Eval基准测试中，使用STAGE生成的训练数据显著提升了Qwen3-4B模型的表现：精确匹配率从31.37%提升至74.27%，值准确率从45.46%提升至90.69%。

Conclusion: 通过利用电子表格作为基准的STAGED数据生成方法，能显著提升LLM在文本到JSON提取任务上的性能，验证了以数据为中心（data-centric）的方法在非结构化文档处理中的有效性。

Abstract: From financial filings to clinical records, legacy industries rely heavily on long, unstructured documents to store high-value information. Reliably extracting this information into structured, machine-readable representations is a key prerequisite to making the contents accessible to automated systems. JSON is a natural target for such structured extraction, yet constructing reliable and scalable text-to-JSON training data remains challenging. To address this gap, we propose STAGE (Spreadsheet-grounded Text-to-JSON Artifact GEneration), a source-grounded data generation pipeline that constructs reports and JSON schema by using LLMs for scalable synthesis while validating ground-truth values against the underlying spreadsheet. Evaluations on STAGE-Eval, our source-grounded benchmark with an 851-example test set, show that STAGE produces stronger training data than existing approaches. This improves Qwen3-4B exact match from 31.37% to 74.27% and value accuracy from 45.46% to 90.69%.

</details>


### [42] [IHUBERT: Vector-Based Semantic Deduplication and Domain-Balanced Pretraining for Persian Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20089)
*Arash Ghafouri,Mahdi Firouzmandi,Hossein Saberi,Mohammad Reza Hasani Ahangar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对波斯语的预训练模型IHUBERT，通过高质量语义清洗数据和优化的BPE分词策略，在多项自然语言理解基准测试中显著提升了模型性能，特别是在问答任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有波斯语预训练语言模型（PLM）存在语料规模小、质量不高，以及评估任务单一（多限于分类和NER）的问题，缺乏针对深度自然语言理解任务的模型。

Method: 1. 构建高质量语料：基于Sepahr-Danesh库，运用多阶段预处理 pipeline（清洗、去重、语义去重、域平衡控制）；2. 自定义分词：训练了139k词汇量的BPE分词器以适应波斯语形态；3. 模型架构：基于RoBERTa-base架构从头预训练（125M参数）；4. 多维评估：在7个任务（NER、情感分析、分类、NLI、问答、关系抽取）上进行了基准测试。

Result: IHUBERT在抽取式问答任务（PQuAD和ParsiNLU-RC）中排名第一；在FarsTail任务中获得最佳结果；在NER和主题分类上表现具有竞争力；关系抽取任务仍有提升空间。 ablation study证明了BPE分词器能有效降低子词碎片化。

Conclusion: IHUBERT通过高质量的预训练语料库和优化的分词策略，在多项波斯语NLU任务（特别是抽取式问答）中取得了优异表现，弥补了现有波斯语模型在规模与评估深度上的不足。

Abstract: Persian pretrained language models (PLMs) are still limited by the scarcity of large-scale, high-quality pretraining corpora and by insufficient evaluation beyond standard classification and NER tasks. We present IHUBERT, a monolingual Persian PLM trained from scratch with the RoBERTa-base encoder (125M parameters) on a 45 GB curated subset of the Sepahr-Danesh collection (about 7-8B tokens). To improve corpus quality and reduce redundancy, we employ a multi-stage preprocessing pipeline that includes normalization, exact and near-duplicate removal, anonymization, and vector-database-based semantic deduplication for distribution balancing control across domains and registers. We additionally train a 139k-vocabulary BPE tokenizer on the full pretraining corpus to better capture Persian morphology and orthographic variation. IHUBERT is evaluated on seven Persian NLU benchmarks covering NER, sentiment analysis, topic classification, NLI, extractive question answering, and relation extraction, using task-standard metrics (entity-level F1, Macro-F1, EM/F1). IHUBERT achieves its strongest gains on extractive QA, ranking first on both PQuAD (F1 88.3542) and ParsiNLU-RC (F1 49.0987), and attains the best result on FarsTail (Macro-F1 0.8350). On NER and topic classification, it remains competitive (e.g., 0.8308 F1 on ParsTwiNER; 0.7953 Macro-F1 on DigiMag), while relation extraction remains the main remaining gap (0.6684 Macro-F1 on PERLEX). A controlled tokenizer ablation on the IHUBERT pretraining corpus shows that BPE yields slightly lower subword fragmentation than WordPiece at matched vocabulary size, supporting our tokenization design. Overall, IHUBERT advances Persian language modeling through semantically curated large-scale pretraining and broad evaluation across both classification and comprehension-oriented tasks.

</details>


### [43] [Self-Preference Is Weak or Absent in Verifiable Instruction-Following Revision: A Four-Model Test Under Genuine Authorship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20093)
*William Guey,Pierrick Bougault*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现大型语言模型在处理自身生成的文本时，并不会因为“自我偏好”而盲目拒绝有效的修正建议，其拒绝行为更多源于对改进方案质量的评估偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型（LLM）在评估自身文本时，是否存在因“自我偏好偏见”而拒绝外部有效修正的倾向，从而导致自我修正能力的局限性。

Method: 利用指令遵循基准（IFEval）作为确定性验证器。首先让模型生成初稿，若初稿违反约束，系统生成一个已通过验证的正确修订建议；随后模型分别以“原作者身份”和“中立的第三方模型身份”对该修订建议进行拒绝或接受的判定，并对比两者拒绝率。

Result: 在测试的4个模型系列中，模型拒绝已验证的正确修订建议的概率与中立模型并无显著统计学差异（差距仅为 -5.1 个百分点）；对于拒绝情况，97% 的原因被归类为针对文本缺陷的判断（即使该判断在客观上是错误的），而非主观偏好。

Conclusion: 在大规模指令遵循任务（IFEval）的评估框架下，没有证据表明模型对自身生成的文本存在“自我偏好（Self-preference）”抑制，模型拒绝有效修正的原因主要在于对生成质量的判断（误判），而非为了维护自我偏好。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly review and revise text, including their own. A documented self-preference bias (models favoring their own generations when acting as judges) raises the question of whether models also resist valid corrections to their own writing. We test this in a setting where "valid" is decided not by another model but by a deterministic verifier: instruction-following revision on IFEval. A model writes a draft; the official IFEval checker confirms the draft violates a constraint and that a candidate edit fixes it; the model then accepts or rejects that edit either as the genuine in-context author or as a fresh model that sees the draft neutrally. Across four mid-tier model families and 85 author-versus-fresh comparisons, we find no detectable self-preference: authors reject verified-good fixes to their own drafts at essentially the same rate as fresh models judging the same drafts (gap -5.1 pp, 95% CI [-12.9, +2.7]). A self-skepticism hint from a smaller pilot did not replicate at scale. The one robust observation is qualitative: when authors do reject a verified-good fix, 97% of their stated reasons are flaw-catching rather than preference, that is, about the character of rejections, not an elevated rate. Effects smaller than ~13 pp cannot be excluded at this sample size.

</details>


### [44] [HydraHead: From Head-Level Functional Heterogeneity to Specialized Attention Hybridization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20097)
*Zhentao Tan,Wei Chen,Jingyi Shen,Yao Liu,Xu Shen,Yue Wu,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HydraHead 提出了一种新的注意力混合策略，即在“头”维度而非“层”维度混合全注意力和线性注意力，通过可解释性精选关键头和特征融合，显著提升了模型处理超长上下文的效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有主流长上下文模型中“层级混合”（Layer-wise Hybridization）未能充分利用架构设计空间的问题，挑战线性注意力与全注意力难以有效集成的固有观念。

Method: 提出 HydraHead 架构：(1) 基于可解释性分析，识别并保留“检索关键头”使用全注意力（FA），其余头使用线性注意力（LA）；(2) 引入尺度归一化融合模块以消除 FA 和 LA 输出的分布差异；(3) 采用三阶段迁移流水线（参数重用与蒸馏）降低训练开销。

Result: 在仅使用 15B tokens 训练的情况下，HydraHead 在 512K 上下文长度任务中比基线提升超过 69%，在 7:1 的 LA 与 FA 比例下即可达到传统 3:1 层级混合模型的长上下文性能，性能逼近原生支持 256K 的 Qwen3.5 模型。

Conclusion: HydraHead 通过在注意力头维度进行混合（Head-level Hybridization），证明了相较于传统的层级混合策略，头级混合能更细粒度地平衡长上下文处理能力与通用推理性能，具有极高的扩展潜力。

Abstract: The quadratic complexity of attention poses a critical bottleneck for long-context processing, spurring interest in hybrid attention designs. Most open-source hybrid models adopt a layer-wise strategy. Yet, prior work has noted the inherent difficulty of integrating Linear Attention (LA) with Full Attention (FA), suggesting that the design space of attention hybridization remains underexplored. To probe this space, we conduct interpretability analysis and observe that layers exhibit block-wise functional similarity, while individual heads within the same layer display distinct functional specialization despite sharing input features. This head-level heterogeneity suggests that the head dimension provides a natural and principled granularity for fusing heterogeneous attention signals. Building on this insight, we introduce HydraHead, a novel architecture that hybridizes FA and LA along the head axis. HydraHead features two key innovations: (1) an interpretability-driven selection strategy that identifies retrieval-critical heads and preserves FA only for them, and (2) a scale-normalized fusion module that reconciles the distributional gap between FA and LA head outputs. By leveraging a three-stage transfer pipeline with parameter reuse and distillation, we achieve high-performance hybrid models with minimal training overhead. Under a unified training setup, HydraHead outperforms other hybrid designs in long-context tasks while maintaining strong general reasoning. With interpretability-driven head selection, it matches a 3:1 layer-wise hybrid's long-context performance at a 7:1 LA-to-FA ratio. Crucially, trained on only 15B tokens, HydraHead achieves over 69% improvement over the baseline at 512K context length, approaching Qwen3.5, a leading model of comparable size with a native context length of 256K. This highlights the significant scaling potential of head-level hybridization.

</details>


### [45] [From Texts to Scores: Tracing the Emergence of Essay Quality Representations in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20152)
*Jiaxu Zuo,Mu You,Kaixin Lan,Tao Fang,Yujia Huo,Henghua Shen,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过对8种LLM进行神经科学级别的分析，揭示了LLM内部存在线性可解码的作文质量表征，并发现了专门负责评分的神经元，从而打开了AES系统的“黑箱”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于大语言模型（LLM）的自动化作文评分（AES）取得了显著进展，但其内部运作机制依然是一个“黑箱”，缺乏对其表征如何编码作文质量的系统性研究。

Method: 通过线性探针（linear probing）、交叉提示泛化（cross-prompt generalization）、降维分析、神经元级分析及干预实验，分析了8种LLM在英语（ASAP++, CSEE）和葡萄牙语（ENEM）数据集上的隐藏层表征。

Result: 1. 作文质量信息在线性上是可访问的，且随层级深度渐进显现；2. 线性探针优于非线性探针，表明质量特征已线性解耦；3. 存在与评分高度相关的“评分神经元”，可通过干预改变评分；4. 神经元分布随作文长度变化，长文更依赖深层特征。

Conclusion: LLMs在内部以线性可解码的形式编码了作文质量信息，这种信息具有跨层级渐进性和跨提示词的泛化能力。此外，研究识别出了特定的“作文评分神经元”，验证了LLM在自动化作文评分（AES）中不仅是表现出色，更具有可解释的内部表征机制。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have substantially transformed Automated Essay Scoring (AES), yet the internal mechanisms underlying LLM-based scoring remain poorly understood. In this work, we systematically analyze the hidden representations of eight LLMs across two English essay datasets (ASAP++, CSEE) and one Portuguese dataset (ENEM). Using linear probing, cross-prompt generalization, dimensionality reduction, and neuron-level analyses, we find consistent evidence that essay quality information is encoded in a linearly accessible form within LLM representations. These representations emerge progressively across layers, remain robust across prompting strategies, and partially transfer across essay prompts despite differences in scoring rubrics. In addition, nonlinear probes provide only marginal and inconsistent improvements over linear probes, suggesting that most essay quality information is already linearly decodable. We further identify individual ``essay scoring neurons'' whose activations strongly correlate with essay scores and whose behavior is sensitive to targeted intervention. Moreover, the layer-wise distribution of these neurons systematically shifts with essay length, with longer essays relying more heavily on deeper layers. Overall, our findings provide evidence that LLMs encode structured representations related to essay quality and offer new insights into the interpretability of LLM-based AES systems.

</details>


### [46] [MedRLM: Recursive Multimodal Health Intelligence for Long-Context Clinical Reasoning, Sensor-Guided Screening, Evidence-Grounded Decision Support, and Community-to-Tertiary Referral Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20164)
*Aueaphum Aueawatthanaphisut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对现有医疗大模型难以应对长程、多模态临床数据的问题，本文提出 MedRLM 框架，通过递归多智能体机制和临床证据图谱内存，实现从独立问答到复杂临床工作流感知的决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型及检索增强生成（RAG）系统难以处理分布在电子病历、医学影像及传感器流中的长上下文异构医疗信息，限制了其在实际临床决策中的应用。

Method: 提出 MedRLM 框架，采用递归代理交互模式，非压缩式处理患者长上下文；构建“临床证据图谱内存”（Clinical Evidence Graph Memory）；引入传感器驱动的递归触发机制和基于不确定性的门控精炼机制。

Result: 设计了包含多模态（文本、影像、传感器）、多智能体协作、递归推理及可审计审计系统的架构，并概述了涵盖 EHR、放射影像、ECG 等多源数据的评估方案，旨在解决长程临床推理和分级诊疗支持问题。

Conclusion: MedRLM 将医疗人工智能从静态问答模式转变为可审计、多模态且具有工作流感知能力的临床决策支持系统，通过递归推理和证据图谱提升复杂临床场景的决策质量。

Abstract: Real-world clinical decision support requires reasoning over heterogeneous and longitudinal patient information rather than answering isolated medical questions. However, current medical large language models and retrieval-augmented generation systems often rely on single-step prompting or retrieval, which can be fragile when clinical evidence is distributed across long electronic health records, medical images, sensor streams, guidelines, and referral constraints. This paper proposes MedRLM, a Recursive Multimodal Health Intelligence framework for long-context clinical reasoning, sensor-guided screening, and community-to-tertiary referral support. Instead of compressing all patient information into one prompt, MedRLM treats the patient case as an external clinical environment that can be recursively inspected, decomposed, retrieved, verified, and synthesized. The framework coordinates specialized agents for clinical text, longitudinal EHR, medical imaging, physiological sensor signals, guideline retrieval, uncertainty auditing, and referral planning. It further introduces a Clinical Evidence Graph Memory to connect patient-specific observations with retrieved evidence, standardized definitions, sensor-derived biomarkers, and referral criteria. A sensor-guided recursive triggering mechanism activates deeper reasoning when abnormal physiological or behavioral patterns are detected, while uncertainty-gated refinement supports clinician review for high-risk or low-confidence cases. We also outline a real-data evaluation design using public and credentialed clinical datasets spanning EHR, radiology, ECG, ICU time series, and referral-proxy outcomes. MedRLM aims to move medical AI from static question answering toward auditable, multimodal, and workflow-aware clinical decision support.

</details>


### [47] [ReNikud: Audio-Supervised Hebrew Grapheme-to-Phoneme Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20179)
*Maxim Melichov,Yakov Kolani,Morris Alper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReNikud 提出了一种利用大规模无标注音频进行弱监督训练并结合字符级对齐约束的新型希伯来语 G2P 模型，克服了传统注音法和直接序列建模的局限，实现了更准确的口语语音转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代希伯来语属于 abjad 写作系统（元音省略，歧义大），现有的基于注音（nikud）的 G2P 方法存在训练数据匮乏、标注成本高、难以捕捉自然口语和词汇重音等局限性，而直接序列到序列的方法在小数据集上表现不佳。

Method: 提出 ReNikud 方法：1. 利用基于音素的 ASR 自动标注流水线从大量无标注音频中获取弱监督数据，捕捉自然口语规范；2. 采用伪注音架构，通过强制字符级对齐（将 IPA 音素映射到每个字符位置）作为归纳偏置，解决 abjad 写作系统的转换难题。

Result: 在现有希伯来语 G2P 基准测试及新构建的 MILIM 口语基准测试中，ReNikud 均超过了当前的最优方法（SOTA）。

Conclusion: ReNikud 通过引入弱音频监督和伪注音架构，有效解决了现代希伯来语 G2P 转换中缺乏标注数据及发音歧义的问题，显著提升了语音合成任务的准确性和自然度。

Abstract: Grapheme-to-phoneme (G2P) conversion for Modern Hebrew is needed for applications like text-to-speech (TTS), but is challenging due to the language's abjad writing system, which leaves vowels largely unwritten, creating substantial ambiguity. Standard approaches first predict vowel diacritics (nikud) to produce International Phonetic Alphabet (IPA) transcriptions, but this is limited: vocalization data is scarce and laborious to produce, it does not specify features such as lexical stress, and it reflects formal grammatical rules rather than everyday spoken pronunciation. Direct sequence-to-sequence IPA prediction, meanwhile, struggles on limited data and fails to exploit the character-level alignment characteristic of abjads. Our method, ReNikud, overcomes these limitations with two key insights: (1) Weak audio supervision via a phoneme-based automatic speech recognition (ASR) pseudo-labeling pipeline on thousands of hours of unlabeled Hebrew audio, yielding phonemic transcriptions that reflect natural spoken norms without manual annotation. (2) A pseudo-vocalization architecture that predicts IPA phonemes at each character position, enforcing character-level alignment as an inductive bias. Results on existing Hebrew G2P benchmarks and the new targeted MILIM benchmark for spoken Hebrew show that ReNikud surpasses previous state-of-the-art methods. We will release our code and trained models to support further work on Hebrew TTS and speech technologies.

</details>


### [48] [Pitch Spelling Jazz Lead Sheets, Solo Transcriptions, Classical Piano and Monophonic Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20198)
*Augustin Bouquillard,Florent Jacquemard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段优化算法，通过联合确定音高拼写、调号与局部音阶，实现了对MIDI格式音乐数据的准确乐谱标注及调性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决音乐转录任务中音高拼写与调性分析的准确性问题，特别是为了支持爵士独奏转录、音乐分析、教学及文化遗产保护。

Method: 提出了一种两阶段优化算法：1. '模态'阶段：通过最短路径搜索最小化乐谱中的变音记号，为每小节确定可能的音阶；2. '调性'阶段：以前一阶段结果为基础，共同优化全局调号和音符名称，以获得最佳乐谱符号表示。此外，定义了新的爵士音阶距离度量。

Result: 该算法在爵士乐谱（如《Real Book》）、爵士独奏及低音线条转录、传统音乐及古典钢琴/单旋律乐谱构成的多样化数据集上进行了评估，表现出良好的适用性。

Conclusion: 该算法通过联合优化音高拼写、调号和局部音阶，能有效处理包括爵士乐、传统音乐和古典音乐在内的多种乐谱，在音乐转录及乐谱处理领域具有广泛的应用价值。

Abstract: We present an algorithm for pitch spelling and key estimation. Given an input in MIDI-like format, containing information on note pitches (expressed in semitones relative to the lowest reference note) and bar boundaries, it estimates the appropriate note names, a global Key Signature, and a local scale for each bar. This related information elements are evaluated jointly during two stages of optimisation. During an initial 'modal' stage, a probable scale is proposed for each bar, minimising the number of accidentals to be printed in the printed score with a shortest-path search. Then, during a second stage called 'tonal', these local scales are used to estimate the Key Signature and note names that would result in the best musical notation for the entire piece. We present evaluations conducted on datasets comprising a variety of digital musical scores: jazz lead sheets taken from the Real Book, transcriptions of recordings of jazz soli and bass lines, traditional tunes, as well as classical scores for piano and monophonic instruments. Our procedure was originally designed for use in music transcription, specifically for building digital collections of jazz solos transcribed from audio recordings, for the purposes of music analysis, teaching and the preservation of cultural heritage. This method should also prove useful for other tasks related to the processing of musical notation. Furthermore, to this end, we have defined new distances between various common jazz scales, which may be of some interest to musicological studies.

</details>


### [49] [CzechDocs: A Multiway Parallel Dataset of Formatted Documents for Minority Languages in Czechia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20212)
*Josef Jon,Ondřej Bojar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CzechDocs 是一个支持格式保留机器翻译评估的多路平行数据集（包含捷克语及其少数民族语言，涵盖 HTML/DOCX/PDF），旨在解决翻译中格式丢失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器翻译系统在处理多格式文档（HTML, DOCX, PDF）时，往往难以同时保持原文的格式信息，缺乏专门用于该领域评估的数据集。

Method: 构建了一个包含 HTML、DOCX 和 PDF 多种格式的多路平行语料库（捷克语及多种少数族裔语言）；开发并发布了配套的评估工具；并在验证集上对比了主流的格式保留翻译方法。

Result: 发布了名为 CzechDocs 的数据集供学术界使用，并确立了对当前格式保留翻译方法的基准评估。

Conclusion: CzechDocs 为格式保留机器翻译的研究提供了宝贵的基准数据集和评估工具，未来将通过共享任务进一步推动文档级机器翻译的发展。

Abstract: We present CzechDocs, a multiway parallel dataset of formatted documents (HTML, DOCX, and PDF) covering Czech and minority languages used in Czechia-primarily Ukrainian and English, with smaller portions of Vietnamese, Russian and other languages. The dataset is designed to support the evaluation of machine translation systems that aim to preserve document formatting during translation. We provide a comparison of the most common approaches to format-preserving machine translation on a validation subset of the dataset. This validation split, together with the evaluation toolkit, is publicly released for further research. A held-out test split will be reserved for a future shared task focused on document-level translation with formatting preservation.

</details>


### [50] [Actionable Activation Directions for Detecting and Mitigating Emergent Misalignment Across Language Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20225)
*Abdul Rafay Syed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现模型中存在可用于减少不安全代码输出的特定激活方向，但这些方向难以在不同模型架构间通用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大语言模型通过不安全代码微调后产生的“涌现式失准”现象，探究这种失准是否对应于跨架构共享、可因果操作的激活空间方向。

Method: 1. 对四个模型家族（Qwen2.5, Gemma-2, Llama-3.2, Ministral）进行相同的不安全代码微调；2. 提取并分析激活空间中的“均值差异方向”（difference-in-means direction）；3. 使用因果干预（Causal steering）和岭回归（Ridge regression）进行迁移测试；4. 通过包含安全代码的对照组评估方向的特异性和因果作用。

Result: 1. 在所有模型中均发现了一个能够以99.6%准确度区分安全与不安全激活的方向；2. 模型内部干预可有效减少21-51点的代码泄露；3. 跨架构迁移虽然能抑制部分不安全行为，但缺乏特异性（即随机方向也表现相似）；4. 发现存在“供体”（Gemma/Qwen）与“受体”（Llama）的不对称迁移拓扑结构。

Conclusion: 通过线性方向进行跨架构的对齐修正存在局限性。虽然模型内部存在可操作的“不安全代码”激活方向，但跨模型的可迁移方向缺乏特异性。因此，建议采用针对特定模型的探测（probing）技术来进行安全审计和修正。

Abstract: Fine-tuning language models on insecure code induces emergent misalignment with poorly understood internal structure. We investigate whether this misalignment corresponds to a causally actionable activation-space direction shared across architectures. Across four instruction-tuned model families (Qwen2.5-1.5B, Gemma-2-2B, Llama-3.2-1B, Ministral-3-3B) finetuned identically, a difference-in-means direction achieves 99.6% separation of aligned and misaligned activations at each model's final layer. Causal steering by subtracting this direction reduces code spillover by 21-51 points, while a secure-code control confirms content specificity. Cross-architecture transfer via ridge regression maps yields large behavioral suppression (up to 46 points) but fails specificity controls as random and orthogonal directions perform comparably. We identify a two-tier specificity structure: within-model directions are causally specific and actionable; cross-model directions are causally real but non-specific. An asymmetric transfer topology emerges, with Gemma and Qwen acting as geometric donors and Llama as a receiver. These findings define the limits of linear cross-architecture correction and recommend within-model probing for auditing.

</details>


### [51] [The Register Gap: A Meaning Intelligence Framework for Nigerian Public Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20255)
*Celestine Achi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为了解决AI在理解尼日利亚公共话语时出现的“语境缺失”问题，研究提出了包含九个评估维度的MIF框架，实验证明该框架能显著提升大模型对复杂言语（反讽、隐意、语域）的精准判定能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有尼日利亚语言基准测试（如NaijaSenti等）仅依赖简单的三分类情感分析，无法解析尼日利亚公共话语中复杂的语境及隐藏意图的问题，旨在解决AI在处理该类语境时的“上下文缺失”缺陷。

Method: 提出了Meaning Intelligence Framework (MIF)，包含九个衡量维度（语域、表面情感、真实意图、反讽、隐晦亚文本、风险等级、标注置信度、说话人情绪、推荐行动）；构建了包含标准英语、尼日利亚英语、尼日利亚皮钦语和代码混合语的30项对比数据集；评估了Gemini 2.5 Flash模型在零样本（zero-shot）与模式提示（schema-informed）条件下的表现。

Result: 在使用MIF模式提示后，模型在语域分类准确率上提升了40个百分点（从33.3%提升至73.3%），综合“意义智能得分”提升了5.4分；具体在隐晦亚文本识别及战略行动建议方面均取得了超过10个百分点的显著提升。

Conclusion: MIF框架通过引入语境感知的九维评估范式，能够显著提升大语言模型对复杂尼日利亚公共话语的理解能力，特别是在处理语域识别和隐晦亚文本方面。该研究表明，针对性模式的引入可以有效弥补AI在处理多语言、多语域话语时的“语境缺失”问题。

Abstract: We introduce the Meaning Intelligence Framework (MIF), a nine-dimension annotation and evaluation schema for Nigerian public discourse that separates surface sentiment from true communicative intent. Existing benchmarks for Nigerian languages, including NaijaSenti and AfriSenti, treat sentiment classification as a three-way polarity task (positive, negative, neutral). We argue that the dominant failure mode of AI systems on Nigerian discourse is not translation failure but context failure: the same utterance carries opposite pragmatic force depending on speaker, audience, and situation. The MIF operationalises this insight across nine scored dimensions: register, surface sentiment, true intent, irony, coded subtext, risk tier, annotator confidence, speaker emotion, and recommended communications action. We construct a 30-item calibration dataset spanning Standard English, Nigerian English, Nigerian Pidgin, and code-mixed registers, and evaluate a frontier language model (Gemini 2.5 Flash) under zero-shot and schema-informed prompting conditions. The headline finding is the Register Gap: zero-shot register classification accuracy is 33.3%, rising to 73.3% (+40 points) when the model receives the MIF schema in-context. The composite Meaning Intelligence Score increases by 5.4 points (73.2 to 78.6) under schema-informed prompting, with the largest practical gains in register identification, coded-subtext detection (+10 points), and strategic action recommendation (+10.3 points). We release the framework specification, annotation guidelines, and the 30-item public calibration set to support reproducibility, while retaining a private holdout corpus for contamination-protected evaluation.

</details>


### [52] [PsyScore: A Psychometrically-Aware Framework for Trait-Adaptive Essay Scoring and ZPD-Scaffolded Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20287)
*Wei Xia,Jin Wu,Haoran Shi,Xiangyu Wang,Chanjin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PsyScore是一种融合心理测量学理论与大模型能力的自动作文评分框架，通过一致的潜在能力表征，实现了可解释的精准评分与基于学生水平的自适应教学反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动作文评分（AES）系统未能有效结合评分与教学反馈：基于神经网络的评分系统通常缺乏可解释性，而基于大模型的反馈往往忽视学生的个体能力差异。

Method: 提出了PsyScore框架，包含三个核心模块：1. Trait-Adaptive Neural IRT Scorer（将GPCM模型引入神经网络，实现具有心理测量学解释性的能力评估）；2. ZPD-Scaffolded Feedback Generator（基于近侧发展区间(ZPD)理论，根据学生能力水平自适应生成反馈）；3. Multi-Perspective Feedback Evaluation Strategy（利用成对偏好判断和修订模拟评估反馈质量）。

Result: 在ASAP++数据集上的实验表明，PsyScore不仅达到了极具竞争力的评分准确度，而且在提供符合教学逻辑的反馈方面表现更优。

Conclusion: PsyScore通过引入心理测量学指导，成功将自动作文评分与个性化教学反馈统一在一个框架下，在实现高精度评分的同时，提供了更符合教学法的反馈策略。

Abstract: Effective Automated Essay Scoring (AES) are expected to support both reliable assessment and actionable instructional feedback. However, existing approaches often treat scoring and feedback as separate components: neural scoring models provide limited interpretability, while Large Language Model (LLM)-based feedback is typically insensitive to learners proficiency levels. To address this fragmentation, this work proposes PsyScore, a psychometrically-aware framework that integrates diagnostic assessment with instructional scaffolding through a shared latent ability representation. PsyScore comprises three key modules: a Trait-Adaptive Neural IRT Scorer that incorporates the Graded Partial Credit Model (GPCM) into a neural architecture, enabling the precise estimation of student ability while maintaining psychometric interpretability, a ZPD-Scaffolded Feedback Generator, which conditions multi-agent feedback strategies on the diagnosed ability parameter to adapt instructional focus across different proficiency levels, and a Multi-Perspective Feedback Evaluation Strategy that assesses feedback quality via pairwise preference judgements and student revision simulations. Experiments on the ASAP++ dataset demonstrate that PsyScore achieves competitive scoring performance while providing more pedagogically aligned feedback.

</details>


### [53] [CATCH-ME if you RAG: a dataset of Contextually Annotated multi-Turn Counterspeech against Hate and Misinformation Exchanges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20369)
*Helena Bonaldi,Genoveffa Martone,Marco Guerini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对在线仇恨言论与虚假信息重叠的问题，发布了首个多语言、专家标注且支持RAG系统的事实溯源对话数据集，旨在通过提供高质量示例显著提升现有大模型生成反击言论的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线仇恨言论与虚假信息常相互重叠，但现有NLP研究多将其割裂。现有的反击言论（Counterspeech）数据集规模有限、仅限单轮且多为单语，无法满足多轮、多语言环境下对高质量事实引导的需求。

Method: 构建了一个大规模、多语言（五种语言）、专家标注的对话数据集，该数据集涵盖了多种边缘群体所遭受的仇恨言论与虚假信息。数据集特别结合了外部事实核查资源，并提供文档及片段级别的标注，以优化检索增强生成（RAG）系统的应用。

Result: 成功发布了一个高质量的、支持多轮交互和事实溯源的跨语言数据集，填补了当前研究在仇恨言论与虚假信息重叠领域的空白，能够支撑更具说服力和事实依据的模型训练与评估。

Conclusion: 该研究通过提供首个多语言、专家标注且带事实依据的数据集，为对抗仇恨言论与虚假信息重叠提供了有力支撑，推动了基于检索增强生成（RAG）的劝导性反击言论研究。

Abstract: Online hate speech and misinformation frequently overlap, yet NLP research has mainly treated them in isolation. While LLMs represent a scalable solution for assisting humans in the generation of counterspeech for both threats, zero-shot models frequently generate repetitive and vague responses, underscoring the need for high-quality examples to steer model generation. However, existing counterspeech datasets against the overlap of hate and misinformation are scarce and limited to single-turn English dialogues, while real-life interactions span across multiple turns and languages. To bridge this gap, we introduce the first large-scale, expert-curated, multilingual dataset of dialogues tackling the intersection of hate and misinformation. To ensure factual grounding, the dialogues are also anchored in verified external knowledge (i.e., fact-checking articles and NGO reports) and include document- and chunk-level span annotations, making it directly applicable for RAG systems. Covering five languages and targeting hate directed at seven marginalized groups, this novel resource enables the training and evaluation of more persuasive, factually grounded counterspeech models.

</details>


### [54] [Your Mouse and Eyes Secretly Leak Your Preference: LLM Alignment using Implicit Feedback from Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20482)
*Haw-Shiuan Chang,Jeffrey Gomez,Mehul Patwari,Aryan Sajith,Hamed Zamani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出利用用户在交互过程中的隐式行为（如鼠标和眼动）作为大模型对齐的奖励信号，显著优于传统的仅依赖显式人类偏好标注的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于显式反馈的RLHF方法存在标注成本高昂且忽略了互联网领域至关重要的隐式反馈（Implicit Feedback）的问题。

Method: 构建了 IFLLM 数据集，通过采集59名参与者与LLM交互时的鼠标轨迹和眼动数据，训练了一个结合隐式反馈的奖励模型，并应用于DPO（直接偏好优化）对齐八个大模型。

Result: 基于隐式反馈的奖励模型将文本奖励模型的准确率从55%提升至64%；在应用DPO后，大模型的响应质量提升幅度提高了近三倍。

Conclusion: 通过利用隐式反馈（鼠标轨迹和眼动数据）构建的奖励模型，能够显著提升大语言模型的对齐效果，证明了隐式反馈在RLHF中具有比单一文本反馈更高的价值与潜力。

Abstract: To align a Large Language Model (LLM), most existing methods collect explicit human feedback and train a reward model to predict the human preference based on the response text. These existing methods have two key limitations. First, the users rarely provide explicit feedback for LLM responses, which makes the high-quality preference annotation expensive to collect. Second, the methods do not leverage implicit human feedback, which has proven vital to the economic moats of Internet giants. To quantify the value of implicit feedback, we build a new dataset called IFLLM, which collects 1336 multi-turn questions from the 59 Mechanical Turk workers, their mouse trajectories, and eye gazing points to the LLMs' responses from their webcams. IFLLM shows that the users have very diverse types of gazing behavior and mouse trajectories. Our reward model based on the implicit user feedback boosts the accuracy of the text-based reward model from 55% to 64% and nearly triples the relative response quality improvements after applying the DPO to eight LLMs, demonstrating the value of implicit feedback in the wild. Our data collection website, dataset, and codes can be found at https://github.com/themehulpatwari/llm-implicit-feedback/.

</details>


### [55] [Beyond Global Replanning: Hierarchical Recovery for Cross-Device Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20487)
*Shu Yao,Yuhua Luo,Qian Long,Jingru Fan,Zhuoyuan Yu,Yuheng Wang,Lin Wu,Yufan Dang,Huatao Li,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对多设备智能体任务执行中的故障恢复困难，本文提出了H-RePlan框架，通过分层策略管理和跨层故障抽象，实现了高效的本地与全局两级故障修复，有效提升了跨设备自动化任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多设备智能体在任务执行失败时，缺乏对设备本地策略空间的建模，往往采用粗粒度的重试或重规划，无法区分“可本地修复的故障”与“需跨设备重规划的故障”，导致效率低下且不稳健。

Method: 提出了H-RePlan框架：1. 使用统一的API-CLI-GUI接口；2. 为每台设备配备多种可互换的执行策略；3. 引入跨层故障抽象（compact cross-layer failure abstraction），将设备本地恢复与编排层全局重规划解耦。同时提出了HeraBench基准测试来评估故障修复能力。

Result: 在HeraBench基准测试中，H-RePlan在任务完成率、指令遵循能力和完美通过率方面均显著优于基准方法，同时降低了实现成功所需的Token成本。

Conclusion: 分层恢复机制（Scope-aware hierarchical recovery）对于实现稳健的多设备智能体执行至关重要，能有效提升任务完成率并降低计算成本。

Abstract: Real-world computer-use tasks often span multiple applications and devices, requiring agents to coordinate heterogeneous environments under dynamic runtime failures. Existing multi-device agent systems support task decomposition and cross-device assignment, but recovery remains largely coarse-grained: when execution fails, they typically retry the same strategy, reassign the subtask, or revise the global plan, without systematically modeling the device-local strategy space. This limits their ability to distinguish failures that can be repaired within the current device from those that require cross-device replanning. We propose \textbf{H-RePlan}, a hierarchical replanning framework for multi-device agents with unified API--CLI--GUI execution. H-RePlan equips each device with interchangeable execution strategies and separates device-local strategy recovery from orchestrator-level global replanning through a compact cross-layer failure abstraction. To evaluate this capability, we introduce \textbf{HeraBench}, a fault-injected benchmark that constructs cross-device workflows over Linux and Android devices and injects strategy- and device-level failures. Experiments show that H-RePlan substantially outperforms single-strategy and coarse-grained multi-device baselines, achieving higher completion, instruction adherence, and perfect-pass rates while reducing the token cost required for reliable end-to-end success. These results demonstrate that scope-aware hierarchical recovery is essential for robust multi-device agent execution.

</details>


### [56] [StylisticBias: A Few Human Visual Cues Drive Most Social Biases in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20527)
*Shaghayegh Kolli,Timo Cavelius,Nafiseh Nikeghbal,Samantha Dalal,Jana Diesner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究者发布了StylisticBias数据集，通过控制变量法揭示了多模态大模型对特定视觉线索（如年龄、体型、时尚风格）的社会偏见，并发现模型在语义相关任务中对这些属性高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态偏见研究难以区分外观特征（如肤色、衣着）与身份差异，导致难以理解哪些具体的视觉线索在驱动模型作出特定的社会评价，因此迫切需要一种细粒度的、受控的偏见评估工具。

Method: 构建了一个包含500个基础人脸及约25K个单属性变体的受控基准数据集StylisticBias。通过保持身份（identity）不变，逐一改变视觉属性，评估了6个主流MLLM在25种社会判断场景下的偏见响应，并量化了特定视觉线索对判断结果的影响。

Result: 实验表明：1. 年龄和体型对身份层面的判断影响最大；2. 时尚风格等视觉线索导致了最大的属性级偏差；3. 约15种特定属性导致了80%的偏差贡献；4. 当判断任务与外观在语义上相关时（如社会经济地位），模型的偏差表现最为强烈。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了多模态大模型（MLLM）在社会判断中存在显著且集中的偏见，偏见主要由特定的视觉属性（如年龄、体型、时尚风格）驱动。StylisticBias作为一种受控的基准测试，对于评估和缓解多模态模型中的细粒度偏见具有重要意义。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in personally and societally consequential settings, yet the visual cues that shape how these models judge people remain poorly understood. Prior work often compares different (groups of) individuals, making it difficult to separate appearance effects from identity differences. We introduce StylisticBias, a controlled benchmark for evaluating attribute-level social bias in MLLMs. We generate 500 photorealistic base faces and create about 50 single-attribute variations per face, producing about 25K images. This design keeps identity fixed and changes one visual attribute at a time. It lets us measure how specific cues shift model judgments. We evaluate six MLLMs across 25 binary social judgment scenarios. We find that age and body type dominate identity-level effects, while fashion style and other visual cues drive the largest attribute-level shifts. We further find that about 15 attributes account for nearly 80\% of the total variation, showing that bias is concentrated in a small set of visual cues. Sensitivity is strongest in judgments that are semantically aligned with appearance, especially socioeconomic and style-related judgments. We release StylisticBias as a benchmark for fine-grained bias evaluation in multimodal models. Code and dataset: https://github.com/timo-cavelius/StylisticBias and https://hf.co/datasets/shaghayegh/stylistic-bias-dataset.

</details>

### [57] [A Topos-Theoretic Interpretation of Blockchain Systems: Sheaves of Consensus and the Logic of Decentralized Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19519)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 传统区块链模型忽视了共识机制的本质，本文提出使用拓扑斯理论（层论）作为形式化描述区块链系统的原生数学语言，从而更贴切地建模分布式环境下的共识与全局真值演变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的区块链形式化模型（如有限状态机）无法准确建模区块链最核心的特性——去中心化环境下的共识构建，常将其作为实现细节排除在模型之外。该文旨在寻求一种更能反映区块链“局部一致性与全局真值构建”本质的新型数学语言。

Method: 提出将区块链系统的核心——即“去中心化共识与全局状态一致性”——纳入数学建模框架，并引入拓扑斯理论（特别是层论 Category of Sheaves）作为新的形式化描述工具。

Result: 论证了拓扑斯理论不仅能够涵盖传统计算模型，还能通过层（sheaves）的视角处理分布式环境下的信息聚合与共识问题，为区块链提供了更严谨、本质的数学基础。

Conclusion: 拓扑斯理论（Topos Theory）是描述区块链共识机制和分布式系统全局真值构建的原生数学语言，能够有效解决传统有限状态机模型在处理去中心化共识时的局限性。

Abstract: The predominant formal models for blockchain systems, particularly smart contracts, have largely been drawn from the classical theory of computation, with the finite state machine (FSM) or labeled transition system serving as the primary conceptual tool. However, the FSM relegates the most difficult and novel aspect of a blockchain -- the achievement of consensus in a decentralized environment -- to a complex, often messy, implementation detail that lies outside the formal model itself. But the process of consensus is not an ancillary feature; it is the very essence of the computational phenomenon. To model it faithfully, a new mathematical language is required. The central thesis of this work is that topos theory, the theory of categories of sheaves, provides the native mathematical language for systems defined by local consistency and the construction of global truth.

</details>

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文认为传统图模型不足以描述现代复杂分布式系统，提出利用数学中的“层理论（Sheaf Theory）”进行建模，并利用“层拉普拉斯算子（Sheaf Laplacian）”实现更高效的分布式数据融合与共识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分布式系统具有高维、异构、多模态及复杂上下文相关性，传统简单图模型在表示此类系统时表现出重大的表达力缺陷，无法满足复杂系统分析的需求。

Conclusion: 层理论（Sheaf Theory）为复杂现代分布式系统提供了比传统简单图模型更具表达力的数学框架，且层拉普拉斯算子（Sheaf Laplacian）是处理分布式感知网络中数据融合与共识生成的有效机制。

Abstract: We argue here that traditional network models, which are overwhelmingly based on the mathematical construct of a simple graph, are fundamentally insufficient for capturing the complexity of modern distributed systems. Such systems are characterized by heterogeneous agents with diverse capabilities, high-dimensional and multi-modal data streams, and intricate, context-dependent relationships that cannot be adequately described by a simple connection or a scalar weight. The limitations of these classical models necessitate a new mathematical language, one with far greater expressive power. We have found that sheaf theory provides us with such a language. Moreover, we show that the sheaf Laplacian is a suitable mechanism for data fusion and establishing consensus within distributed sensing networks.

</details>


### [59] [SAC: Disaggregated KV Cache System for Sparse Attention LLMs with CXL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19746)
*Ruiyang Ma,Teng Ma,Junru Li,Hantian Zha,Xuchun Shang,Qingda Hu,Zheng Liu,Xinjun Yang,Tao Ma,Guojie Luo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SAC是首个专为稀疏注意力模型优化的解耦KV缓存系统，利用CXL的细粒度存取特性，解决了传统RDMA方案在长上下文推理中的效率瓶颈，显著提升了推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM长上下文推理导致内存容量成为瓶颈。传统基于RDMA的解耦内存方案对稀疏注意力模型无效，因为它们强制加载完整的KV缓存，造成严重的传输瓶颈和内存浪费。

Method: 提出SAC系统，利用CXL（Compute Express Link）的低延迟、缓存行粒度存取语义，在推理过程中按需仅获取所需的top-k KV条目，而非全量加载。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V3.2模型和SGLang框架的评估下，相比基于RDMA的基线，SAC实现了2.1倍的吞吐量提升，TTFT（首字延迟）降低了9.7倍，TBT（每字延迟）降低了1.8倍。

Conclusion: 针对稀疏注意力模型，基于CXL的解耦KV缓存（SAC）优于传统的基于RDMA的方案，成为更卓越的基础设施。

Abstract: The scaling of LLMs toward long-context inference has shifted the primary serving system bottleneck from computation to memory capacity. Traditional solutions for dense attention models rely on RDMA-based disaggregated memory pools, which perform coarse-grained fetching of the entire prefix KV cache from remote storage to local memory before decoding. However, this approach is fundamentally inefficient for emerging sparse attention models. While only a small fraction of KV entries are active during decoding, these systems still fetch the full KV cache locally, leading to severe transmission bottlenecks and local memory wastage. To address this, we propose SAC, the first efficient disaggregated KV cache system optimized for sparse attention models. By leveraging the low-latency, cache-line granularity load/store semantics of Compute Express Link (CXL), SAC fetches only the required top-k KV entries on demand during inference. Evaluations on DeepSeek-V3.2 using SGLang show that SAC achieves 2.1x higher throughput, 9.7x lower TTFT, and 1.8x lower TBT compared to RDMA-based baselines, establishing CXL-based disaggregation as the superior infrastructure for emerging sparse attention models.

</details>


### [60] [Certified Euclidean-Residue Minimal-Alignment Switch Decompositions for Three Edge-Disjoint Hamiltonian Cycles in Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19832)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文为非互质EJ网络提供了一种全新的、基于Cayley几何的局部切换演算方法，通过数学公式实现了边不交哈密顿圈的最小切换分解，并使用代数证据而非计算穷举证明了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非互质（gcd(a,b)>1）EJ网络中的边不交哈密顿圈（EDHC）分解问题。传统方法往往依赖复杂的排程或计算穷举，缺乏直观的代数公式表征。

Method: 摒弃了传统的矩形表示和交换调度，转而在自然的Cayley几何中开发了一种“局部切换演算（local switch calculus）”。该方法将前两个哈密顿圈的构建简化为最小的d-1个分量间切换，并通过剩余边补集直接获得第三个因子。

Result: 实现了对欧几里得剩余家族的紧凑、公式驱动的最小切换分解；通过四个核心逻辑步骤（分量标签坍缩、锚点抵消、提升切换代表元的非碰撞性、拓扑连接的补集关联）完成了理论证明。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种针对非互质Eisenstein-Jacobi (EJ)网络的闭式、最小切换分解方法，并通过代数证书证明了其互补关联性，避开了依赖有限穷举的证明。

Abstract: Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) networks are degree-six quotient-lattice interconnection networks. For a generator $α=a+bρ$, let $N=a^2+ab+b^2$ and $d=\gcd(a,b)$. If $d=1$, the three natural unit directions already give three edge-disjoint Hamiltonian cycles. If $d>1$, each unit direction splits into $d$ cycles and the EDHC problem becomes a cycle-splicing problem. Existing non-coprime EJ decompositions prove existence by using a rectangular representation and exchange schedules. This paper develops a different, local switch calculus in the natural Cayley geometry. The first two Hamiltonian cycles are built using the minimum possible $d-1$ intercomponent switches each, and the third factor is obtained as the unused edge complement. The contribution is deliberately not a new existence theorem for all non-coprime EJ networks; rather, it is a compact, formula-driven, minimal-switch decomposition for Euclidean-residue families whose complement incidence is proved symbolically. The proof separates four ingredients: component-label collapse, anchor cancellation, noncollision of lifted switch representatives, and connected complement incidence. No infinite-family theorem in this manuscript is proved by finite witnesses or by computational enumeration. The theorem scope is stated for the parameter ranges where an algebraic complement-incidence certificate is written down. Tables and CSV data are used only to verify and reproduce the formulas, never as proof of an infinite-family theorem.

</details>


### [61] [Fault-Tolerant Shared-Relay Communication in Circulant Interconnection Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19833)
*Bader Albader,Galal Hassan,Mohamed R. Al-Mulla*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种优化有向循环互连网络中继容错性的设计框架，通过计算优化生成器集合，显著提升了网络在两跳传输中的负载均衡与故障生存能力，优于传统区间生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决有向循环互连网络中如何配置生成器以满足两跳故障容错（即对于每一对终端，必须存在足够数量的共享中继节点）的问题，并定量评估度数-冗余度之间的权衡关系。

Method: 该研究提出了一个基于循环差多重性条件的网络设计框架，涵盖了对最差情况下共享中继多重性 $R(n, m)$ 的分析、中继表预处理与查找算法、对抗性和随机故障下的容错保证，并通过对 526,539 个生成器集合进行大规模计算实验进行了验证。

Result: 该研究建立了度数-冗余度分析景观，证明了传统区间生成器存在结构性缺陷；优化的设计方案可以将故障容错性能提升至理论下界的 1.16 到 1.63 倍。

Conclusion: 生成器集合的选择对循环互连网络的故障容错能力（中继可恢复性）至关重要。经过优化的阈值设计方案能实现近乎理论最优的容错性能，而传统的区间生成器在即使具有较大度数时也可能存在结构性失效。

Abstract: Circulant interconnection networks provide symmetric addressing, compact generator descriptions, and uniform local connectivity. This paper maps a degree--redundancy landscape for a fault-tolerant two-hop primitive in directed circulants: given $n$ nodes and degree budget $m$, how large can the worst-case shared-relay multiplicity $R(n,m)$ be? A node is a shared relay for an ordered terminal pair if it has outgoing links to both terminals; an $f$-relay-fault-tolerant circulant requires at least $f+1$ such relays for every pair. The underlying feasibility condition is a cyclic difference-multiplicity condition, which we use as a mathematical tool rather than claim as a new object. The contribution is the network-design framework around this tool: the parameters $R(n,m)$ and $D_f(n)$, a negative theorem for interval circulants, relay-table preprocessing and lookup algorithms, adversarial and random failure guarantees, load-balance scope, certified upper-bound interpretation of heuristic designs, exact small-$n$ calibration, a software lookup-versus-search microbenchmark, and a reproducible study of 526,539 generator sets. The results show that generator choice critically determines worst-case relay survivability: optimized threshold designs achieve $f$-relay-fault tolerance within about $1.16$--$1.63$ of the counting lower bound, while standard interval generators can fail structurally even at much larger degrees.

</details>


### [62] [Multi-Orientation Edge-Minimum Repair for Non-Redundant Fault-Tolerant Broadcasting in Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19834)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了用于密集 EJ 网络的 EJ-MOEM 广播修复算法，通过重新配置广播树结构，实现了在有故障情况下非冗余、低深度（$t+1$ 或 $t+2$）的一对多消息广播修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决密集 Eisenstein-Jacobi (EJ) 网络在面对节点或链路故障时，实现非冗余、一对多广播树高效修复的问题，特别是如何保持结构性并最小化修复深度。

Method: 提出名为 EJ-MOEM 的多方向边最小修复算法。通过评估六边形广播树方向、选择故障感知候选者、收缩故障剪除树为健康组件，并利用外部组件交叉修复边进行重连，从而生成健康的生成树。

Result: 证明了在给定的方向下，$c-1$ 条外部修复边是必要的且充分的；证明了单故障修复深度不超过 $t+1$，双故障不超过 $t+2$；通过从 $t=2$ 到 $t=200$ 的详尽仿真验证了算法的正确性和效率。

Conclusion: EJ-MOEM 是一种在密集 EJ 网络中实现高效、鲁棒的一对多广播修复的有效方法。理论上证明了其修复深度限制（一故障 $t+1$，二故障 $t+2$），并通过了广泛的仿真验证。

Abstract: Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) networks are degree-six algebraic interconnection networks whose finite quotient geometry is naturally represented by a hexagonal axial-coordinate ball. This paper studies non-redundant one-to-all broadcast repair in the dense EJ network generated by $α=(t+1)+tω$, where $t$ is the network diameter. We propose EJ-MOEM, a multi-orientation edge-minimum repair method that evaluates a constant-size family of hexagonal broadcast-tree orientations, selects a fault-aware candidate, contracts the fault-pruned tree into healthy components, and reconnects these components using external component-crossing repair edges. The resulting structure is a rooted spanning tree of the healthy subgraph: every healthy node receives the message exactly once, no faulty node is used, and the original healthy tree components are preserved. We prove that, for a chosen orientation whose fault-pruned component graph is connected, exactly $c-1$ external repair edges are necessary and sufficient, where $c$ is the number of healthy components. We also prove a depth-certificate theorem for EJ coordinate-reduction trees: every one-fault placement admits a repair of depth at most $t+1$, and every two-fault placement admits a repair of depth at most $t+2$. The proof uses the three-strip representation of EJ hexagons, a sector-suffix attachment lemma, a non-adjacent-sector separation lemma, and a six-direction shielding classification for paired cuts. Extended validation includes exhaustive one- and two-fault enumeration for $t=2,\ldots,12,14,16,18$ (up to $N=1027$ and 525,825 two-fault placements at $t=18$), structured theorem-critical tests through $t=30$, and large random tests through $t=200$, all with 100\% success and no violation of the theorem.

</details>


### [63] [EVM Workloads in the Wild: Evidence for Multi-Dimensional Gas Metering, State Growth, Delayed Execution, and Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19869)
*Lioba Heimbach,Kushal Babel,Jason Milionis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了EVM现行单标量Gas计费模型的局限性，通过对比以太坊L1与Base L2，证明资源消耗组合的不稳定性和状态敏感性会显著影响交易的可预测性与执行效率，进而论证了多维Gas计量和状态增长显式定价的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EVM当前的Gas计量模型假设执行环境是稳定的（资源组合固定、状态漂移影响可忽略），但随着区块链发展，这种假设已不再成立。研究旨在量化该假设的失效程度及其对交易执行的影响。

Method: 通过对2025年以太坊(L1)和Base(L2)进行大规模追踪级(trace-level)测量，抽样分析每日3000个区块；将交易拆解为操作码级资源消耗（执行Gas、内在Gas、退款、状态增量）；并通过在旧状态上重新执行2025年9月的交易，测试状态灵敏度。

Result: 1. 资源组合极度不稳定：Base存储读取和计算占比高，以太坊侧重存储写入；2. 状态膨胀问题严重：Base的状态增长远超以太坊（456GB vs 38GB）；3. 执行结果不稳定：Base上46%的交易在不同状态下Gas预估存在差异（以太坊为13.9%），且状态变化导致存储访问模式发生偏移，影响访问列表效能和并行执行效率。

Conclusion: 现有EVM的单标量计费模型无法准确反映真实资源消耗及状态增长成本，必须转向多维Gas计费机制，并对永久性状态增长进行显式定价，以提升交易的可预测性和用户体验。

Abstract: Gas metering on EVM-compatible blockchains assumes that execution conditions are stable: that the resource mix is constant enough to justify collapsing execution costs into a single scalar with fixed relative prices, and that state drift between submission and execution does not materially alter a transaction's outcome. We measure the extent to which this assumption fails.
  We present a trace-level measurement study of EVM workloads on Ethereum (L1) and Base (L2) throughout 2025, sampling 3,000 blocks per day per chain. We decompose each transaction into opcode-level execution gas, intrinsic gas, refunds, and persistent state deltas. To measure state sensitivity, we re-execute transactions from September 2025 on older states and record how gas usage and storage access patterns change.
  We find the resource mix to be far from stable: on Base, storage reads and compute account for 29.2% and 24.3% of execution gas, while Ethereum devotes 34.9% to storage writes. Ethereum's gas limit doubling during 2025 shifted its own profile toward compute-heavier, Base-like patterns. Base also exhibits a higher fraction of cold storage reads (49.7% versus 39.6% on Ethereum). Persistent state growth, a permanent cost priced as a transient one, reaches 456 GB on Base versus 38 GB on Ethereum.
  Execution outcomes are equally unstable: gas estimates vary across nearby historical states for 46.0% of transactions on Base, compared to 13.9% on Ethereum, with especially high sensitivity for MEV and DeFi activity. Storage access patterns also diverge across states, limiting the effectiveness of access lists and complicating parallel execution.
  Our work provides an empirical foundation for multi-dimensional gas metering and explicit pricing of state growth. They show that state-sensitive execution behavior complicates workload estimation, directly affecting transaction predictability and user experience.

</details>


### [64] [Online Dynamic Batching with Formal Guarantees for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19989)
*Dian Li,Zekun Wang,Yaoru Wang,Jiahong Yan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对多模态大模型训练中因样本长度高度不确定导致计算效率低下的问题，ODB 通过在线动态调整批次构建策略，显著提升了训练吞吐量，且无需改变模型架构或进行繁琐的预计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多模态大模型训练中，样本在预处理及补全前后的真实长度差异巨大，导致传统的离线采样器无法准确预估显存占用和GPU负载，造成资源浪费。

Method: 提出了“在线动态批处理”（Online Dynamic Batching, ODB），将批次构建移至预处理、分词及多模态扩展之后，实现了对真实计算成本的准确感知，并通过解决“分布式组对齐问题”（DGAP）确保了分布式训练的对齐和无死锁终止。

Result: 在多个模型（Qwen3-VL）和数据集（UltraChat/LLaVA等）实验中，ODB 相比标准批处理实现了 1.58-4.43 倍的吞吐量提升，同时保持与标准训练相当的指标质量，并超越了现有的离线长度预估基准。

Conclusion: ODB 实现了高性能的在线动态批处理，证明了其在异构数据集上优于传统的离线批处理方法，且无需复杂的预处理缓存或内核修改，具备极高的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Modern LLM training breaks a core assumption behind offline batch samplers: the true training cost of a sample is only observable after preprocessing, augmentation, templating, tokenization, and multimodal visual-token expansion. Unless one pays for a preprocessing- and augmentation-dependent length cache, batch construction is therefore blind to the quantity that determines padding, memory use, and GPU saturation. We introduce Online Dynamic Batching (ODB), a DataLoader-side drop-in system that moves batch formation to this point of accurate observability while preserving DDP step alignment. We formalize this synchronization requirement as the Distributed Group Alignment Problem and prove deadlock-free bounded termination with default join-mode identity coverage and opt-in non-join sample-quota closure. ODB requires no model, optimizer, or attention-kernel changes and is released as online-dynamic-batching with lightweight trainer adapters. Across public 2B/8B Qwen3-VL runs on UltraChat/LLaVA/ShareGPT4o, ODB improves literal emitted-sample throughput vs. fixed-batch Standard by 1.58-2.51x on single-node Full FT/LoRA and 1.71-3.78x on two-node Full FT, with Standard-comparable quality; production MM-Mix reaches 4.43x. Against GMT/BMT offline token-budget oracles, ODB is within 15% on UltraChat/LLaVA and faster on high-CV ShareGPT4o: 2.24-2.39x single-node Full FT/LoRA and 3.06-3.69x two-node Full FT. Together, ODB occupies the online/drop-in regime for high-heterogeneity LLM fine-tuning: large throughput gains at Standard-comparable quality, formal DGAP guarantees, and no length-cache precompute or kernel rewrites.

</details>


### [65] [ARGUS: Production-Scale Tracing and Performance Diagnosis for over 10,000-GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20374)
*Jiasheng Zhou,Longbin Zeng,Clavis Chen,Ruiming Lu,Qinwei Yang,Leyi Ye,Ray Ying,Key Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ARGUS 是一种面向超大规模 GPU 集群的低开销（<2%）、全时可观测性系统，通过分层采集与高效数据压缩实现了故障的实时自动化定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具存在矛盾：粗粒度监控无法定位根因，而传统细粒度 Profiler 开销过大（5%-30%）且数据量过高，无法在 10,000+ GPU 的生产集群中进行全时常驻部署。

Method: 1. 分层观测：解耦 CPU 调用栈、框架语义逻辑和 GPU 内核执行；2. 极致压缩：采用数据流压缩技术，将原始内核事件压缩约 3,700 倍（从 10MB 降至 2.7KB）；3. 渐进式诊断：通过迭代级、阶段级和内核级的多层级自动化分析，自动定位异常窗、慢节点及退化算子。

Result: 1. 将全时监控的总开销控制在 2% 以下；2. 在 10,000+ GPU 的生产集群中稳定运行超过六个月；3. 成功识别了多种复杂性能异常（如计算拖慢、流水线气泡放大、FlashAttention JIT 停顿等）。

Conclusion: ARGUS 证明了在超大规模（10,000+ GPU）生产环境中实现低开销、细粒度的全时监控是可行的，不仅能有效检测隐藏的性能异常（如计算延迟、链路劣化等），还显著降低了运维压力。

Abstract: Large-scale LLM training requires always-on, fine-grained observability for effective performance diagnosis at scale. Coarse resource monitors alone cannot localize root causes, and fine-grained profilers incur prohibitive (5%-30%) overheads and massive trace volumes, making always-on deployment impractical in large production clusters.
  We propose ARGUS, a low-overhead, fine-grained, always-on tracing and real-time analysis system for training workloads in 10,000+ GPU-scale production clusters. ARGUS decomposes observation along the training call hierarchy into CPU call stacks, framework semantics, and GPU kernel execution, with always-on collection under a combined overhead of less than 2%. It builds a unified data pipeline and compresses raw kernel events by approximately 3,700x from 10 MB to 2.7 KB per rank per step. Its progressive diagnosis framework automatically isolates anomalous windows, straggler ranks, and degraded kernels through iteration-time, phase-level, and kernel-level analysis. Deployed for over six months on a 10,000+ GPU production cluster, ARGUS has supported continuous fail-slow detection and performance optimization. Our case studies further demonstrate its effectiveness across representative anomalies, including compute stragglers, link degradation, pipeline-bubble amplification, FlashAttention JIT stalls, and compute stragglers masked by communication symptoms.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [66] [Computational Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19361)
*Lucius E. J. Bynum,Rajesh Ranganath,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“计算可识别性”概念，将因果识别从理想化的理论推导转向基于有限计算资源的搜索过程，有效解决了小样本、混合数据及复杂因果结构下的可识别性评估难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果识别理论主要关注极端情况下的“理论可识别性”（基于假设、无限数据和渐近性质），缺乏对有限样本、模糊图形判据及混合数据类型等复杂实际场景的计算处理能力。

Method: 提出了一种基于计算搜索过程的框架，通过设定先验分布和误差容忍度，利用算法 empirically 寻找满足特定条件的估计量，从而评估因果效应的可识别性。

Result: 通过实验证实，该框架能够有效处理小样本、模糊图形条件、混合观测/干预数据以及反事实估计问题，为因果识别提供了更具操作性和灵活性的一套工具。

Conclusion: 本文提出了“计算可识别性”框架，认为在有限计算资源和样本限制下，可识别性应被重新定义为一种基于搜索过程的实证性质，而非仅局限于渐近理论下的理想化推导。

Abstract: Identification conditions describe the computability of a target query or parameter of interest as a function of the type and amount of information available. In causal identification, this information is often expressed in the form of a causal graph, and data are observed or collected for some subset of variables in the graph. Target queries may be for a single effect alone or for a class of effects in a given model. The derivation of an identification algorithm then defines mathematically the process by which the desired causal effect(s) can be uniquely determined, theoretically, in expectation. Identifiability in expectation, or 'theoretical identifiability,' generally assumes asymptotic properties, infinite data, or other mathematically idealized conditions. In this paper, we explore a fundamental distinction between this theoretical, idealized notion of identifiability and a proposed alternative that is computation-bound. The framework we propose - 'computational identifiability' - is to instead define a finite computational search procedure for an empirical estimator. If this process finds an estimator empirically, within a desired error tolerance, then identifiability is satisfied, conditional on the specified assumptions of the search (i.e., a prior distribution over the parameters) and conditional on the search procedure itself. Through several experiments, we demonstrate how this framework allows us to answer fine-grained, practical identification questions, such as identification with small finite samples, with ambiguous graphical criteria, with mixed observational-interventional data, and across counterfactual data and estimands. Code is available at https://github.com/lbynum/metadentify.

</details>


### [67] [Low-Energy Reduced RISC-V Instruction Subset Processor for Tsetlin Machine Inference at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19964)
*Chanda Gupta,Sanidhya Bhatia,Shaurya Priyadarshi,Himani Panwar,Rishad Shafik,Sudip Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Tsetlin Machine推理优化的专用RISC-V处理器，通过指令集和电路层面的裁剪，在实现高能效与高性能的同时，保留了RISC-V架构的灵活性，使其优于二值神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Tsetlin Machine专用硬件加速器多依赖于紧耦合接口和微码编程，灵活性与易用性较差。研究旨在通过模块化RISC-V架构设计，提升TM工作负载在边缘计算中的性能与能效，同时解决可编程性不足的问题。

Method: 提出了一种面向TM推理的专用RISC-V微处理器架构与设计流程。通过指令分析（Instruction Profiling）精简指令集，并裁剪数据路径与控制路径，在保持可编程性的前提下实现了软硬件的高效适配。

Result: 实验表明，该设计在多个数据集上相较于二值神经网络（BNN）显著提升了准确率（如在CIFAR-2上达到88.18%对比BNN的60.0%），推理执行时间减少高达98%，平均能耗降低了29.7倍。

Conclusion: 该专用RISC-V架构证明了在保持可编程性的同时，针对Tsetlin Machine（TM）推理进行体系结构优化，可显著提升边缘AI系统的能效比与性能，是优于BNN的可行方案。

Abstract: Tsetlin Machine (TM) is a logic-based machine learning approach that relies on simple bitwise operations and finite-state automata, which makes it attractive for edge AI deployments. Recent work has focused on co-processor and accelerator designs based on Tsetlin Machines (TMs). Although these designs achieve high performance, they typically depend on tightly coupled interfaces, microcode-style programming, and external host processors, limiting flexibility and ease of programming. In this work, we present a domain-specific RISC-V microprocessor architecture and design flow tailored for TM inference. Leveraging the modular structure of RISC-V, we design a reduced instruction subset processor that retains programmability while targeting improved performance and lower energy consumption for TM workloads. Instruction profiling is employed to guide instruction reduction, followed by datapath and control path simplifications tailored to TM inference. Both the baseline RV32IM core and the proposed reduced core are evaluated across multiple datasets and compared with Binarized Neural Networks (BNNs), which serve as a hardware-efficient baseline due to their reliance on bitwise operations during inference. Results show that TM achieves comparable or higher accuracy (e.g., up to 88.18% on CIFAR-2 compared to 60.0% for BNN) while reducing execution time by up to 98% across multiple datasets. Furthermore, the proposed design achieves an average $29.7\times$ reduction in energy consumption, demonstrating its effectiveness for programmable and efficient edge AI systems.

</details>


### [68] [UltraQuant: 4-bit KV Caching for Context-Heavy Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20474)
*Inesh Chakrabarti,David Limpus,Aditi Ghai Rana,Bowen Bao,Spandan Tiwari,Thiago Crepaldi,Ashish Sirasao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UltraQuant 通过 4-bit KV Cache 量化技术及 AMD GPU 专有内核优化，显著缓解了智能体任务中的缓存压力，在大幅降低延迟的同时有效提升了推理吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文智能体任务中，频繁复用的长前缀对 KV Cache 造成了巨大压力；如何在满足算力资源利用率的前提下，解决 KV Cache 的存储瓶颈并提升服务吞吐量是当前 LLM 推理的关键挑战。

Method: 1. 将 4-bit KV Cache 量化策略应用于多轮智能体工作负载；
2. 采用不对称 K/V 处理、Walsh-Hadamard 旋转及块级缩放等技术增强量化鲁棒性；
3. 开发 UltraQuant：基于 FP8 查询和 FP4 KV 张量的量化方案，并利用 AMD CDNA4 架构的 scaled-MFMA 指令及优化后的 decode-attention 内核进行加速。

Result: 在长上下文多轮智能体工作负载中，相比 FP8 KV 基准，UltraQuant 将缓存受限的后期轮次 P50 首字延迟降低了 3.47 倍（全轮次平均降低 2.3 倍），并将输出吞吐量提高了 1.63 倍。

Conclusion: UltraQuant 通过对 KV Cache 进行 4-bit 量化及针对 AMD GPU 的架构优化，在长上下文多轮智能体任务中，实现在保持任务质量的同时，大幅降低首字延迟并提高吞吐量，证明了 4-bit 量化在大规模智能体推理中的实用性。

Abstract: Context-heavy agents place unusual pressure on the key-value (KV) cache: long prefixes are reused across many short turns, while concurrency determines whether the serving system can keep GPUs utilized. We study 4-bit KV-cache compression for this setting, using TurboQuant-style rotation and codebook quantization as a quality anchor and vLLM FP8 KV caching as the deployment anchor. We report three contributions. First, we frame 4-bit KV caching around multi-round agent workloads where task quality, cache residency, and serving throughput must be measured jointly. Second, we describe the practical design choices needed to make the 4-bit path robust, including asymmetric K/V treatment, Walsh-Hadamard rotation, QJL removal, and block-scale variants. Third, we present serving optimizations on AMD GPUs, including optimized decode-attention kernels and UltraQuant, an FP4 approximation path that uses FP8 queries, FP4 KV tensors, UE8M0 group scales, and native scaled-MFMA support on CDNA4. On a long-context, multi-turn agentic workload, UltraQuant cuts P50 time-to-first-token by 3.47x in the cache-pressured late rounds (2.3x across all rounds) and raises output throughput by 1.63x over the FP8 KV baseline.

</details>


### [69] [When to Trust, How to Distill: Multi-Foundation Model Guidance for Lightweight, Robust Scientific Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19363)
*Rupasree Dey,Abdul Matin,Nathan Orwick,Yao Zhang,Shrideep Pallickara,Sangmi Lee Pallickara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对物理科学领域时间序列预测中基础模型计算成本高及分布偏移的问题，论文提出了GUARD蒸馏框架，通过上下文路由与不确定性门控机制，将基础模型知识迁移至高效的边缘侧轻量级模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型在物理科学领域存在严重的数据分布偏移问题，且计算成本过高，不适用于边缘计算场景，因此需要一种方法将基础模型中的通用结构知识蒸馏至轻量级模型中。

Method: 提出了Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (Guard) 框架：1) 上下文路由器（Contextual Router）：根据输入特征动态选择最相关的教师模型；2) 不确定性门控温度机制（Uncertainty-Gated Temperature）：根据教师模型的置信度动态调整蒸馏强度，避免错误引导。

Result: 在气象、生态碳通量、土壤湿度和能源电网四个领域表现优异，显著降低了均方根误差（RMSE）；在该方法处理的最困难实例中，有28.5%表现优于全局最优的基础模型。

Conclusion: GUARD框架实现了从分布偏移的重型基础模型中有效提取知识的能力，为资源受限的边缘环境提供了高效、高精度的科学预测方案。即使在基础模型零样本预测效果不佳的情况下，其蕴含的潜变量结构知识仍可作为有效的修正信号。

Abstract: The deployment of Time-Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) in physical sciences is hindered by a critical trade-off: while these models encode rich, universal temporal dynamics, they suffer from severe distributional misalignment when applied zero-shot to specific scientific domains, and their computational cost prohibits deployment in edge-computing sensor networks. We address a fundamental challenge: How can we extract latent structural knowledge from misaligned foundation models (FM) to train lightweight, specialized forecasters? We propose Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (Guard), a novel framework that reframes multiteacher distillation as an instance-wise decision process with two adaptive mechanisms: (1) a Contextual Router that dynamically selects the most relevant teacher based on local input statistics, exploiting complementarity across diverse foundation models; and (2) an Uncertainty-Gated Temperature mechanism that acts as a "circuit-breaker," automatically attenuating distillation strength when teacher confidence diverges from domain reality. We evaluate our proposed lightweight framework on four climate-critical domains: meteorology, ecosystem carbon flux, soil moisture, and energy grids. Our method significantly reduces RMSE relative to a fixed-weight multi-teacher distillation baseline, successfully distilling knowledge from pretrained FMs (teachers) even when they exhibit suboptimal zero-shot accuracy due to distribution shift between the original and target data domains. We demonstrate that these domain-misaligned teachers can still serve as critical correctives, outperforming the globally superior FMs on 28.5% of the hardest instances. Ultimately, this enables high-precision scientific forecasting suitable for resource-constrained edge deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/RupasreeDey/GUARD-KDD2026.

</details>


### [70] [Closing the Social-Semantic Gap: SPSD for Edge-Based Prompt Compression in Cloud LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19364)
*Abhinit Sen,Ajeet Kumar,Manaranjan Pradhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPSD通过在边缘设备上对用户提示词进行预处理和语义压缩，在保证大模型回复质量的前提下，显著降低了云端推理的Token开销与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型推理中的前缀（Prefill）阶段能源成本不断增加，而用户提示词中包含大量对机器学习推理而言信息冗余的社交沟通语言（如礼貌用语、重复等），导致了“社会-语义鸿沟”（Social-Semantic Gap）。

Method: 提出了一种名为SPSD（Sentiment Preserving Semantic Distillation）的边缘管道。该方法在发送至云端大模型（LLM）之前，利用本地边缘部署的4位量化小型语言模型（SLM，如Gemma-2-2B-Instruct）对用户提示词进行压缩，去除其中低信息量的社交修辞（即“社会-语义鸿沟”），并采用基于规则的门控机制保护安全关键任务。

Result: 在实验中，平均每次调用可节省99.9个输入Token；LLM-as-judge评估显示其响应质量与原始提示词相比处于“非劣效”（Non-inferior）水平；单次调用估计可节省70-270 uWh的能源。

Conclusion: SPSD验证了在边缘设备上进行提示词蒸馏（Prompt Distillation）是一种有效的策略，能够在不显著牺牲响应质量的前提下，有效降低云端大型语言模型的输入Token成本及能源消耗。

Abstract: The prefill stage of Large Language Model (LLM) inference is a growing contributor to cloud-scale energy cost. Many consumer-support and conversational prompts contain social scaffolding: politeness markers, apologetic preamble, repetition, and rapport-building language that is important for human communication but carries low marginal information for machine reasoning. We call this discrepancy the Social-Semantic Gap. We present SPSD (Sentiment Preserving Semantic Distillation), an edge-based pipeline that compresses user prompts using a 4-bit quantised Small Language Model before transmission to a cloud-deployed LLM. Evaluation on a 248-prompt corpus using Gemma-2-2B-Instruct (Q4_K_M) as the SLM and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct as the cloud evaluation model yields a mean input token saving of 99.9 tokens per distilled call, with all 146 distilled calls yielding positive savings. Response quality, assessed by blind LLM-as-judge scoring across 121 pairs, is non-inferior to the raw path within a pre-specified 1-point margin on a 15-point rubric; the judge awarded 43 percent ties, 28 percent distilled wins, and 29 percent raw wins. Cosine similarity is mixed: mean 0.682, median 0.712, with 54.1 percent of pairs above the 0.70 reference threshold. Safety-critical domains are conservatively routed to passthrough via rule-based gates. Per-call net energy saving is estimated at 70-270 uWh under stated assumptions. SPSD shows that on-device prompt distillation can reduce cloud LLM input-token cost while preserving response quality within a practical non-inferiority margin.

</details>


### [71] [Performance Analysis and Optimization of 3D Generative Diffusion Models across GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19365)
*Jeeho Ryoo,Yongchan Jung,Muhammad Ali Khaliq,Weidong Zhang,Jiatong Han,Byeong Kil Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决 3D MRI 扩散模型在大规模 GPU 部署中的效率问题，本文通过性能剖析找出了卷积与 gemm 内核的瓶颈，并利用 TF32 和 channels-last 布局优化显著提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在 3D MRI 合成中表现出色，但因计算开销巨大（数百次 U-Net 推理）且内核行为高度异构，导致其在 GPU 上的部署面临严峻的资源瓶颈。

Method: 针对 Med-DDPM 模型在三代 NVIDIA 架构上进行了详尽的性能分析，涵盖内核运行时、指令混合、内存访问、Warp 活动及性能瓶颈评估；并实施了 TF32 Tensor Core 激活和 3D channels-last 布局优化。

Result: 优化后 SM 周期及动态指令数最高减少 100 倍，Tensor Core 利用率提升约 7 倍（1.45x 至 9.98x），IPC 提升 7%。

Conclusion: 通过架构感知优化（TF32 Tensor Core 激活和 3D channels-last 布局），显著降低了 SM 周期与指令开销，大幅提升了 Tensor Core 利用率与 IPC，在保持 MRI 合成质量的同时解决了资源受限问题。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become essential for high-fidelity 3D MRI synthesis, yet their deployment remains constrained by substantial GPU resource demands arising from hundreds of U-Net evaluations per sample and a highly heterogeneous kernel behavior. This paper performs a comprehensive performance analysis of the state-of-the-art medical diffusion model, Med-DDPM, across three generations of NVIDIA architectures to study kernel-level runtime breakdowns, instruction-mix characteristics, memory system utilization, warp-level activities, and profiler priority-score estimates. We show that training is overwhelmingly dominated by cuDNN convolution and implicit-GEMM kernels, with inefficiencies arising from memory-access patterns, tensor-layout conversions, and limited Tensor Core utilization. Guided by these insights, we evaluate two architecture-aware optimizations TF32 Tensor Core activation and a 3D channels-last layout and demonstrate that they reduce SM cycles by up to 100x, cut dynamic instructions by 100x, raise Tensor Core utilization from 1.45 to 9.98x, and increase IPC by 7% on A100, all without degrading synthesis quality.

</details>


### [72] [Information Lattice Learning as Probabilistic Graphical Model Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19366)
*Haizi Yu,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文阐明了信息格学习（ILL）与概率图模型（PGM）的内在联系，揭示了 ILL 本质上是基于商变量空间学习可解释的约束因子图，并证明了其重构过程与最大熵原理的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了从数学上明确信息格学习（ILL）与概率图模型（PGM）之间的联系，阐述 ILL 如何从概率质量函数中学习可解释的概率规则，并建立其在统计建模中的理论地位。

Method: 通过在划分格（partition lattice）上交替进行投影（学习抽象规则）和提升（将规则映射回信号域，即重构联合分布），并引入 Shannon 熵作为 lifting 标准，将其与最大熵模型和对数线性因子图关联起来。

Result: 证明了 ILL 学习到的概率规则等同于商变量上的边缘约束；揭示了在最大熵原则下，ILL 的提升过程可导出对数线性因子图；澄清了信息格的结构（细化/粗化关系）与贝叶斯网络（条件独立性）的区别。

Conclusion: ILL 是一种针对商变量的、可解释的基于约束的因子图结构学习方法，它通过划分格将信号抽象化，并利用最大熵原理实现从规则到概率分布的重构。该研究确立了 ILL 与 PGM 之间的理论联系，为符号-概率混合学习提供了新视角。

Abstract: Information lattice learning (ILL) learns interpretable rules of a signal by alternately projecting the signal onto a partition lattice that encodes a hierarchy of abstractions and lifting selected rules back to the signal domain. When the signal is a probability mass function, we show the probabilistic rules learned by ILL admit a natural probabilistic graphical model (PGM) interpretation and develop this interpretation in detail. A partition in ILL induces a deterministic quotient variable, and a rule is the marginal law of that quotient variable. A rule set is therefore a collection of marginal constraints over interpretable abstractions. General lifting is the feasible family of all joint distributions satisfying those constraints, while special lifting chooses a maximum-ignorance reconstruction, implemented in ILL by an L2 uniformity principle closely related to maximum entropy. Under a Shannon-entropy lifting, the same constraints yield a log-linear factor graph whose factors are indexed by learned abstractions. The information lattice itself, however, is not a Bayesian network: its edges encode refinement and coarsening of abstractions, not conditional dependence. Thus ILL is best viewed as structure learning for interpretable constraint-based factor graphs over quotient variables. This view clarifies how ILL relates to graphical models and maximum entropy models, while suggesting new directions for inference, identifiability, and hybrid symbolic-probabilistic learning.

</details>


### [73] [Weibull Weight-Scale Parameter Evolution under AdamW Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19367)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了AdamW训练中Transformer权重Weibull分布尺度参数λ的变化机制，通过动力学力的分解解释了λ的演变规律，并提出了一种基于稀疏检查点的对齐力重构方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对AdamW训练过程中权重分布及其尺度参数λ随时间演变（增长、过冲、弛豫）的深层动力学机制解释。

Method: 1. 利用两参数Weibull框架建模Transformer权重分布；
2. 推导出AdamW更新中权重平方范数的三力分解（对齐力、注入力、衰减力）；
3. 引入样条位移法（spline displacement method）从稀疏检查点重构对齐力。

Result: 1. 揭示了对齐力占据约88-94%的增长期力预算；
2. 解释了饱和阶段对齐力与衰减力的平衡机制；
3. 样条位移法在无优化器动量的情况下，准确率达92-94%。
