GRPO improves IW skill-step accuracy only from 18.5\% to 20.5\%, leaves BrowseComp+ essentially unchanged, and underperforms trivial frequency priors on key source-domain metrics. We therefore present the method as a diagnostic study: trajectory mining can expose inspectable skill structure, but the current boundary detector, orderless segment representation, and offline reward model are insufficient for reliable cross-domain policy improvement.

</details>


### [254] [Rethinking Shrinkage Bias in LLM FP4 Pretraining: Geometric Origin, Systemic Impact, and UFP4 Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20381)
*Qian Zhao,Kunlong Chen,Changxin Tian,Zhonghui Jiang,Haitao Zhang,Chaofan Yu,Peijie Jiang,Mingliang Gong,Jia Liu,Ziqi Liu,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了当前FP4训练中常用的E2M1格式存在会导致不稳定的“收缩偏差”，并提出了利用均匀4-bit网格（E1M2/INT4）的UFP4训练配方，在大型模型预训练中实现了更稳定的学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流FP4训练方案（如NVIDIA Blackwell等）多依赖E2M1格式，但该格式在LLM预训练中存在固有的数学局限性（偏向性和不稳定性），难以有效提升训练性能。

Method: 1. 理论分析：识别非均匀格式（E2M1）中的“收缩偏差”及其在多层网络中的乘法累积效应。2. 实验设计：对比E2M1与均匀格式（E1M2/INT4）在RHT下的量化质量。3. 提出UFP4：一种对所有三个训练GEMM应用RHT，并仅对dY使用随机舍入的均匀4-bit训练配方。4. 实验验证：在Dense 1.5B、MoE 7.9B和MoE 124B模型上进行长程预训练实验。

Result: UFP4在各项预训练实验中，相对于强基准（E2M1-based baselines），表现出更低的BF16损失劣化，并在扩展律分析中验证了其优越性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，非均匀格式（如E2M1）存在“收缩偏差”（Shrinkage Bias），导致训练不稳定。相比之下，均匀格式（E1M2/INT4）能更好地利用随机哈达玛变换（RHT）。因此，作者提出了UFP4训练配方，并建议未来的AI加速器应将E1M2/INT4类均匀4-bit网格作为一等训练原语。

Abstract: FP4 training promises substantial reductions in memory and computation cost for LLM pretraining, yet current FP4 hardware paths and recipes, including NVIDIA Blackwell/Rubin-class systems and AMD MI350-series GPUs, remain centered on E2M1 data elements. In this study, we identify a fundamental limitation of that choice: non-uniform formats such as E2M1 inherently suffer from Shrinkage Bias, a systematic negative rounding error caused by the geometric asymmetry of their representable bins. We show that this bias accumulates multiplicatively across layers and is amplified by the Random Hadamard Transform (RHT), providing a unified explanation for the training instability observed in existing E2M1-based FP4 recipes. In contrast, uniform grids (E1M2/INT4) bypass this grid-geometry error and better convert the improved bucket utilization from RHT into higher quantization quality. Based on this finding, we propose UFP4, a uniform 4-bit training recipe that applies RHT to all three training GEMMs while restricting stochastic rounding to dY alone. On Dense 1.5B, MoE 7.9B, and MoE 124B long-run pretraining, UFP4 consistently achieves lower BF16-relative loss degradation than strong E2M1-based baselines, supported by scaling-law analysis and ablation studies. Our results suggest that future accelerators should support E1M2/INT4-style uniform 4-bit grids as first-class training primitives alongside E2M1.

</details>


### [255] [Interpretable Sperm Morphology Classification via Attention-Guided Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20438)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Reza Khoshkangini,Thomas Ebner,Lars Johansson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力机制的深度学习框架（EfficientNet-B0 + CBAM），在提高精子形态分类准确性的同时，通过 Grad-CAM++ 增强了模型的可解释性，更利于临床辅助诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的精子形态分析深度学习模型缺乏可解释性，限制了其在临床环境中的应用，需要开发更透明、更精准的分析工具。

Method: 结合预训练模型 EfficientNet-B0 与卷积块注意力模块 (CBAM)，并利用 Grad-CAM++ 技术进行模型决策的可视化分析。

Result: 在 SMIDS 和 HuSHem 数据集上分别达到了 90.2% 和 93.9% 的准确率（宏平均 F1 分数分别为 0.913 和 0.948），性能优于 SimpleCNN 和标准 EfficientNet-B0。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的基于注意力机制的深度学习框架实现了对精子形态的高精度分类，并具备良好的可解释性，是临床自动化精子分析的有效工具。

Abstract: Male infertility is a major cause of couple infertility, often linked to abnormal sperm morphology. While deep learning models offer automated analysis, most lack interpretability, limiting their clinical adoption. This study proposes an attention-guided deep learning framework for sperm morphology classification. We combine a pretrained EfficientNet-B0 with a Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) to focus on key areas of the sperm head, improving both accuracy and interpretability. Evaluated on the SMIDS and HuSHem public datasets, our model achieves accuracies of 90.2% and 93.9% (macro F1 scores of 0.913 and 0.948), outperforming SimpleCNN and standard EfficientNet-B0. Furthermore, we use Grad-CAM++ visualizations to highlight features influencing the model's decisions. The results demonstrate that this accurate and transparent framework is a practical tool for automated sperm analysis in fertility clinics.

</details>


### [256] [Context-Aware Hierarchical Bayesian Modeling of IVF Laboratory Environmental Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20459)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Reza Khoshkangini,Pia Saldeen,Lars Johansson,Thomas Ebner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过提取IVF孵育器微环境的动态时序特征，并利用层级贝叶斯模型实现跨区域知识共享，显著提高了IVF妊娠率的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的IVF妊娠率模型主要依赖患者个人变量，忽略了实验室高分辨率环境数据的动态特性，导致预测能力受限。

Method: 1. 特征工程：从实验室传感器数据中提取了55个具备上下文感知的时序特征（如热稳定性、温湿度协同、压力峰值持续时间及压力后恢复速度）。2. 模型构建：采用层级贝叶斯Beta回归（Hierarchical Bayesian Beta Regression），通过部分池化（partial pooling）技术实现跨诊所数据的知识迁移，同时保留特定诊所的基准线。

Result: 1. 在亚洲诊所数据上，基于新特征的模型将预测误差降至1.27%（传统原始平均值法为3-5%）。2. 在北欧诊所的测试中，模型达到R2=0.86，在35-39岁年龄组中实现了64%的误差降低。

Conclusion: 研究证明，精细化的实验室环境动态特征包含具有临床意义且可迁移的预测信号，能够显著提升IVF妊娠率的预测准确性。

Abstract: IVF pregnancy rates are routinely modeled using patient-level variables, while high-resolution laboratory environmental data remain underutilized. We show that this is a missed opportunity. Rather than relying on raw sensor averages, we engineer 55 context-aware temporal features, including rolling thermal stability, simultaneous temperature-humidity adherence, peak stress duration, and post-stress recovery speed, that capture the dynamics of incubator microenvironments. On 61 weeks of data from an Asian IVF clinic, these features reduce cross-validated prediction error to 1.27%, compared to 3-5% for raw averages. We then train a hierarchical Bayesian Beta regression model that shares environmental effects across an Asian and a Northern European clinic via partial pooling, while preserving site-specific baselines. On held-out data from the Northern European clinic, the model achieves R2 = 0.86 and a 64% error reduction for the 35-39 age group over a naive baseline, demonstrating that structured environmental monitoring contains clinically meaningful, transferable signal.

</details>


### [257] [What Do Safety-Aligned LLMs Learn From Mixed Compliance Demonstrations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20508)
*Sihui Dai,Mann Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验揭示了大语言模型在上下文学习中处理有害演示的机制，发现模型的合规倾向受到演示顺序、内容类型及偏好优化训练的协同控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往研究表明上下文演示可导致大语言模型越狱，但模型如何具体解析不同类型的合规演示，以及这些演示如何驱动有害行为的内部机制尚不明确。

Method: 通过混合良性与有害的合规演示，测试关于演示组合如何驱动有害合规的三种假设，并在四个模型上评估其在不同演示顺序、内容类型以及训练阶段下的表现。

Result: 1. 良性与有害演示不可互换，良性演示对有害合规的影响因模型而异；2. 偏好优化是防止良性演示增加有害合规的关键阶段；3. 演示顺序存在强烈的近因效应（recency bias）；4. 模型对拒绝机制的处理存在差异（有的模型在拒绝时仍遵循格式，有的则覆盖所有信号）。

Conclusion: 模型对上下文演示（in-context demonstrations）的解析并非简单的模仿，而是受到演示内容、顺序以及模型训练方法（特别是偏好优化）的复杂交互影响。这为理解模型如何被越狱提供了更深入的机制性洞察。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that in-context demonstrations can jailbreak language models, but it remains unclear how models interpret different types of compliance demonstrations. We study this by mixing benign compliance demonstrations (non-harmful request, helpful response) with harmful compliance demonstrations (harmful request, helpful response) and testing three hypotheses about how demonstration composition drives harmful compliance. Across four models, we find that benign and harmful demonstrations are not interchangeable: benign demonstrations can either reduce or increase harmful compliance depending on the model. We further show that preference optimization is the critical training stage that prevents benign demonstrations from increasing harmful compliance, that demonstration ordering exhibits strong recency bias, and that models differ in how refusal interacts with in-context learning: some adopt demonstrated formatting even when refusing, while others override all in-context signals upon refusal. Taken together, this work moves beyond showing that demonstration-based jailbreaking works to characterizing how it works: what models extract from compliance demonstrations depends on demonstration content, ordering, and training methodology.

</details>


### [258] [Multi-LCB: Extending LiveCodeBench to Multiple Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20517)
*Maria Ivanova,Pavel Zadorozhny,Rodion Levichev,Ivan Petrov,Adamenko Pavel,Ivan Lopatin,Alexey Kutalev,Dmitrii Babaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Multi-LCB 将 LiveCodeBench 扩展至 12 种编程语言，通过解决语言单一性盲点，揭示了大模型在跨语言代码生成上的严重短板和性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LiveCodeBench (LCB) 仅限于 Python 语言评估，无法衡量大模型在真实软件工程中所需的跨语言编程能力，且未能有效暴露模型针对特定语言的过度拟合现象。

Method: 将现有的 LiveCodeBench (LCB) Python 任务转换为其他 11 种编程语言（共 12 种语言），同时保留 LCB 原有的污染控制机制和评估协议，并以此评估了 24 个主流大模型的指令遵循与推理能力。

Result: 研究发现大模型存在明显的 Python 倾向性，表现出对特定语言的过拟合和数据污染，且在不同语言间的性能存在巨大差异。

Conclusion: Multi-LCB 作为一个严谨的基准测试，揭示了当前大模型在多语言代码生成任务中的显著性能差距、Python 过拟合现象及语言特定的数据污染问题，证实了跨语言评估的必要性。

Abstract: LiveCodeBench (LCB) has recently become a widely adopted benchmark for evaluating large language models (LLMs) on code-generation tasks. By curating competitive programming problems, constantly adding fresh problems to the set, and filtering them by release dates, LCB provides contamination-aware evaluation and offers a holistic view of coding capability. However, LCB remains restricted to Python, leaving open the question of whether LLMs can generalize across the diverse programming languages required in real-world software engineering.
  We introduce Multi-LCB, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs across twelve programming languages, including Python. Multi-LCB transforms Python tasks from the LCB dataset into equivalent tasks in other languages while preserving LCB's contamination controls and evaluation protocol. Because it is fully compatible with the original LCB format, Multi-LCB will automatically track future LCB updates, enabling systematic assessment of cross-language code generation competence and requiring models to sustain performance well beyond Python.
  We evaluated 24 LLMs for instruction and reasoning on Multi-LCB, uncovering evidence of Python overfitting, language-specific contamination, and substantial disparities in multilingual performance. Our results establish Multi-LCB as a rigorous new benchmark for multi-programming-language code evaluation, directly addressing LCB's primary limitation and exposing critical gaps in current LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [259] [FlowEdit: Associative Memory for Lifelong Pronunciation Adaptation in Flow-Matching TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20518)
*Harshit Singh,Ayush Pratap Singh,Nityanand Mathur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowEdit 是一种无需重新训练的 TTS 模型终身修正框架，通过潜空间嵌入扰动与 Hopfield 记忆网络实现高效、实时的专有名词发音纠正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Flow-matching TTS 系统在部署后是静态的，无法在不重新训练的情况下纠正零样本场景下对生僻专有名词的发音错误。

Method: 提出 FlowEdit 框架：1. 将纠错建模为潜空间文本嵌入的扰动（而非模型权重更新）；2. 使用 Modern Hopfield Network 作为内容可寻址的情景记忆库存储修正；3. 推理时利用带相似度门控的软注意力机制实现模糊形态匹配。

Result: 在包含 18 个语系、312 个专有名词的基准测试中，目标词音素错误率（PER）降低了 92.7%，且保持了原有的语音质量，单次修正推理耗时约 15 秒。

Conclusion: FlowEdit 证明了通过在潜空间进行编辑并结合新型联想记忆网络（Modern Hopfield Network），无需重新训练即可高效完成 TTS 系统的终身发音修正，在保持高质量语音的同时显著降低了专有名词的音素错误率。

Abstract: Flow-matching text-to-speech systems achieve remarkable zero-shot quality but remain static after deployment: pronunciation errors on out-of-vocabulary proper nouns persist unless the model is retrained. We introduce FlowEdit, a life-long adaptation framework for frozen flow-matching TTS that learns pronunciation corrections as latent conditioning edits rather than weight updates. When corrective feedback is provided, FlowEdit optimizes a token-level perturbation in the text embedding space, then stores the correction in a Modern Hopfield Network serving as content-addressable episodic memory. At inference, corrections are retrieved via soft attention with a similarity gate, enabling fuzzy morphological matching. On our curated benchmark of 312 multilingual proper nouns across 18 language families, FlowEdit reduces target-word Phoneme Error Rate by 92.7% relative to the zero-shot baseline while maintaining identical general-speech quality. Corrections complete in approximately 15 seconds on a single GPU.

</details>


### [260] [DeepSWIP: Quotient-WMC Counterfactuals for Neural Probabilistic Logic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20526)
*Saimun Habib,Vaishak Belle,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepSWIP 是一种针对 DeepProbLog 的新型、高效反事实推理框架，通过单世界转换简化了计算流程，并在保证精确度的同时显著提升了推理速度并降低了因神经模型偏差导致的预测偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经符号系统（如 DeepProbLog）主要基于关联推理，缺乏对因果反事实推理的支持；传统的反事实推理方法（如 DeepTwin）存在计算开销大、推理效率低的问题。

Method: 1. 引入 DeepSWIP：一种基于单世界反事实语义的 DeepProbLog 扩展；2. 神经具体化（Neural Materialization）：将固定的上下文神经谓词简化为普通 ProbLog 选择；3. 应用单世界干预程序（SWIPs）；4. 通过加权模型计数（WMC）在单一转换后的程序上计算反事实。

Result: 1. 在 MPI3D 数据集上，DeepSWIP 的推理速度比 DeepTwin 快 2.14 倍，且结果保持一致；2. 证明了在正确的作用域随机策略下，使用 AIPW 估计器可以有效消除神经校准退化带来的系统性偏差。

Conclusion: DeepSWIP 提出了一种高效、精确的神经符号反事实推理框架，通过单世界转换（SWIP）和神经具体化，实现了比传统 DeepTwin 方法更快的推理速度，且在偏差纠正（如使用 AIPW 估计器）方面表现优异。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems such as DeepProbLog combine neural perception with probabilistic logic, but standard inference is associational. Counterfactual reasoning additionally requires a causal semantics for interventions and evidence. We introduce DeepSWIP, a single-world counterfactual semantics for DeepProbLog programs. Using neural materialization, we reduce fixed-context neural predicates to ordinary ProbLog choices, apply Single World Intervention Programs (SWIPs), and compute counterfactuals by weighted model counting (WMC) over a single transformed program. Under finite grounding and unique-supported-model assumptions, DeepSWIP is exact relative to the learned materialized FCM. The standard quotient-WMC form of ProbLog conditionals identifies active neural probabilities and explains intervention cleaning, calibration sensitivity, and rare-evidence instability. Experiments on MPI3D confirm the transformation against a DeepTwin construction against 12,000 queries, as predicted and a 2.14$\times$ inference speedup from avoiding the Twin's endogenous duplication. A SUMO HOV experiment shows that neural calibration degradation biases plug-in estimates, while a correctly scoped randomized-policy AIPW estimator removes most first-order bias for population mean and ATE estimands. Code is at https://github.com/saibib/deep_SWIP.

</details>


### [261] [LedgerAgent: Structured State for Policy-Adherent Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20529)
*Md Nayem Uddin,Amir Saeidi,Eduardo Blanco,Chitta Baral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对传统工具调用代理因缺乏显式状态管理而导致的推理不稳定和策略违规问题，LedgerAgent 通过引入独立的任务状态存储（Ledger）和执行前的策略约束检查，显著提升了代理的可靠性和合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准代理缺乏明确的任务状态管理，将信息混合在提示词中会导致“状态推理失败”导致的逻辑错误（如使用过期/缺失信息）以及违反策略约束的工具调用（即逻辑正确但违规的调用）。

Method: 提出一种名为 LedgerAgent 的推理时方法。该方法在传统提示词（Prompt）之外维护一个独立的“分类账”（Ledger）来存储观察到的任务状态（事实、约束等）。在执行可能改变环境的工具调用前，利用该 Ledger 进行策略约束预检查，防止违反业务规则。

Result: 在四个客户服务领域和多种主流模型（开源和闭源）测试中，LedgerAgent 在平均 pass@k 指标上优于标准提示词基准，尤其在严格的多轮一致性指标下提升显著。

Conclusion: LedgerAgent 通过引入显式状态管理和预检查机制，显著提升了工具调用代理在多轮对话以及复杂策略限制下的准确性和合规性。

Abstract: Policy-adherent tool-calling agents in customer-service domains must maintain task states across turns while calling tools and obeying domain policies. Task states consist of relevant facts, identifiers, constraints, and conditions observed through user interaction and tool calls. In standard agents, task states are not represented separately. Observations, tool returns, and policy instructions are placed in the prompt, leaving agents to reconstruct the relevant states from the prompt each time they decide what to do next. This design makes state management implicit, creating two common failure modes. An agent may retrieve the right facts but later ground its decision in stale, missing, or incorrect information; and a syntactically valid tool call may still violate a domain policy that depends on the current task state. We introduce \textsc{LedgerAgent}, an inference-time method for tool-calling agents that maintains observed task states in a separate ledger and renders the states into the prompt. The ledger is also used to check state-dependent policy constraints before environment-changing tool calls are executed, blocking policy violations. Across four customer-service domains and a mixed panel of open- and closed-weight models, \textsc{LedgerAgent} improves average pass\textasciicircum{}k over a standard prompt-based tool-calling approach, with the largest gains under stricter multi-trial consistency metrics.

</details>


### [262] [How Do Instructions Shape Speech? Cross-Attention Attribution for Style-Captioned Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20532)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Wasim Madha,Apoorv Singh,Sameer Khurana,Akshat Mandloi,Sudarshan Kamath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次将DAAM归因框架引入语音扩散模型，揭示了自然语言风格词元如何调控语音合成，发现模型通过特定的深层网络结构和早期扩散步骤实现对基频与能量的精细化控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于自然语言控制（Style-captioned）的语音合成（TTS）系统虽然提供了灵活性，但其内部机制类似于“黑盒”，缺乏对自然语言词元如何具体映射到语音声学特征（如音高、能量）的理解，阻碍了模型的故障诊断与可控性优化。

Method: 将计算机视觉领域的DAAM（扩散归因映射）框架首次适配至语音领域，针对CapSpeech-TTS模型提出了一种跨注意力归因方法。通过分析25层神经网络及24步ODE扩散过程中的逐词热力图（对3600组样本进行实证：120种风格描述×30段文本），量化自然语言词元对声学输出的影响。

Result: 1. 证实了风格词元具有较低的时间方差，符合全局条件化的设计初衷；2. 风格注意力权重与基频(F0)和能量特征正相关；3. 风格引导在扩散采样阶段的早期和神经网络的深层最为显著；4. 注意力熵在第17层达到最小值，此时模型表现出最强的风格选择性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过跨注意力归因分析，揭示了自然语言风格描述在语音扩散模型中控制语音特征的机制。研究表明，模型通过全局风格条件化与局部特征调控相结合的方式实现表达性合成，为语音扩散模型的可解释性与可控性提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Style-captioned text-to-speech systems use natural language to control voice characteristics, but how individual words influence acoustic output remains unclear. Understanding this is critical for diagnosing failure modes and improving controllability in expressive TTS. We propose cross-attention attribution for speech diffusion models, adapting the DAAM framework to the speech domain for the first time, and apply it to CapSpeech-TTS. Our method extracts per-token heatmaps across 25 layers and 24 ODE steps. We analyze 3,600 (style caption, text transcript) combinations comprising 120 style captions conditioning the generation of 30 text transcripts each, revealing how caption tokens shape waveforms. Results show: (1) style tokens have lower temporal variance than content/function tokens, confirming global conditioning; (2) style attention correlates with F0 and energy; (3) style conditioning peaks in early steps and deep layers; (4) attention entropy reaches its minimum at layer 17, co-occurring with the style importance peak, indicating maximal network selectivity at the most style-critical stage. This is the first study of how natural language influences cross-attention in speech diffusion models

</details>


### [263] [Toward Calibrated Mixture-of-Experts Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20544)
*Gina Wong,Drew Prinster,Suchi Saria,Rama Chellappa,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了混合专家（MoE）模型在分布偏移下的校准问题，指出软路由模型仅靠专家校准是不够的，并提出通过对抗性重加权来优化路由聚合后的整体校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究表明专家级校准能提升专家混合（MoE）模型的表现，但缺乏对其在分布偏移条件下如何工作的深入理解，且现有的校准方法在软路由设置中不够有效。

Method: 1. 对 MoE 模型在分布偏移（distribution shift）下的路由机制与专家校准进行理论分析；2. 提出一种对抗性重加权（adversarial reweighting）策略，通过惩罚分布偏移下路由聚合后的校准误差来优化模型。

Result: 该对抗性重加权方法在多个模型类别、预测任务及分布偏移场景中，均证明能提升模型在整体数据及困难子集上的准确率-校准权衡性能。

Conclusion: 专家层面的校准对于硬路由（hard-routed）MoE 模型在分布偏移下保持整体校准是充分的，但对于软路由（soft-routed）模型则不然。提出的对抗性重加权方法能有效改善模型在分布偏移下的准确率-校准权衡。

Abstract: Calibration aligns a model's predictive uncertainty with the frequencies of its empirical outcomes and is important for understanding and trusting reported probabilities. Recent work shows that enforcing calibration at the level of individual predictors can improve ensemble accuracy and calibration, with mixture-of-experts (MoE) models showing strong empirical improvements in particular; however, the conditions under which calibration helps MoE are not well understood. In this work, we study how MoE models behave under distribution shift, focusing on how routing mechanisms interact with expert-level calibration. We show that expert calibration is sufficient to ensure calibration of the overall model under a broad class of distribution shifts in hard-routed models, but is insufficient for calibrating soft-routed models. To address this, we propose an adversarial reweighting that penalizes calibration errors of the routed aggregate under distribution shift, and we demonstrate that it improves the accuracy-calibration tradeoff both on average and on difficult subsets of the data, across model classes, prediction tasks, and distribution shifts.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [264] [REMOP: REmote-Memory-aware OPerator Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19576)
*Shiquan Zhang,Yunhao Mao,Yuqiu Zhang,Gengrui Zhang,Jeyhun Karimov,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: REMOP是一个感知远程内存特性的算子优化框架，通过优化缓冲分区策略以减少网络通信往返次数，显著提升了分析型数据库在内存溢出时的查询效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析型数据库利用远程/解耦内存池扩展容量时，传统以“最小化数据I/O总量”为核心的磁盘溢出策略不再适用，易导致过多的通信往返延迟。

Method: 提出REMOP优化框架，将“传输轮次（transfer rounds）”引入延迟成本模型，并针对阻塞嵌套循环连接（BNLJ）、外部归并排序和外部哈希连接推导了特定的缓冲分区策略。

Result: 在两节点计算-内存测试平台实验中，REMOP在微基准测试中将传输轮次减少了多达97%，算子运行时间缩短了48%；在TPC-H和TPC-DS查询中，平均运行时间分别降低了22.7%和26.4%。

Conclusion: REMOP通过将传输轮次作为核心考量因素纳入算子优化策略，能显著减少远程内存访问的延迟开销，为分析型数据库在内存受限场景下的性能提升提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Remote and disaggregated memory tiers expand the effective memory capacity of analytical database engines, but they also reshape the cost structure of out-of-memory query processing. When an operator spills beyond local DRAM, moving pages to remote memory incurs both data-transfer time and a fixed round-trip latency per transfer. Classical operator analyses and buffer-allocation heuristics primarily target disk spilling by minimizing total I/O volume. Under remote memory, these strategies can be suboptimal because they may trigger excessive transfer rounds. We present REMOP, a remote-memory-aware operator optimization framework that uses transfer-round-aware intra-operator memory policies to improve out-of-memory execution under tight memory budgets. REMOP introduces the number of transfer rounds into the latency cost model and derives operator-specific buffer-partitioning strategies, instantiating the approach for blocked nested-loop join, external merge sort, and external hash join in DuckDB. Our evaluation on a two-node compute-memory testbed shows that REMOP reduces transfer rounds by up to 97% and operator runtime by up to 48% on spill-heavy microbenchmarks, and lowers the average runtime of spilling TPC-H and TPC-DS queries by 22.7% and 26.4% end-to-end.

</details>


### [265] [DeQL: A Decision Query Language for Prescriptive Analytics over Relational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19751)
*Matteo Brucato,Fjodor Kholodkov,Soren Little,Jakob Mayer,Duc Nguyen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DeQL 是一种将 SQL 扩展至决策优化领域的语言，允许用户通过声明式语法定义候选空间、约束和优化目标，使关系型数据库具备直接执行最佳决策的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统 SQL 仅能进行数据查询而无法直接表达和执行复杂决策优化（如资源分配、调度等）的问题，旨在简化决策问题的建模过程，实现数据与决策引擎的紧密结合。

Method: 在标准 SQL 基础上引入了两个关键结构：CREATE CANDIDATES（定义选项候选空间）和 DECIDE（声明决策变量、约束和目标函数），由查询引擎负责解析并执行底层的优化求解策略。

Result: 提出了一个完整的 DeQL 语言规格说明，支持多种决策场景，并涵盖了不确定性优化、在线模型评分以及时间和质量界限控制等高级功能。

Conclusion: DeQL 成功将决策优化集成到 SQL 中，通过核心构建块（CREATE CANDIDATES 和 DECIDE）实现了描述性和声明性的决策建模，为数据库驱动的自动化决策奠定了基础。

Abstract: DeQL (Decision Query Language) extends SQL to express decision queries: given options drawn from relational data, constraints from policy, and a measurable objective, a DeQL query computes the best course of action. Two constructs carry the extension: CREATE CANDIDATES, which defines the space of options from relational sources, and DECIDE, which declares decision variables, named constraints, and an objective over them. The design follows SQL's principles: the user states what to optimize while the engine chooses how to solve it, every query consumes and produces relations, and the structure of a problem stays visible to the engine. This document specifies the language (its design principles, syntax, formal grammar, and execution model) with examples spanning subset selection, allocation, assignment, scheduling, and decisions at multiple levels of aggregation, and extensions for optimization under uncertainty, inline model scoring, and time- and quality-bounded solving. It is the first version of the specification; the language is under active development, and this version fixes the core constructs on which later revisions will build.

</details>


### [266] [Policy-aware Vector Search: A Vision for Fine Grained Access Control in Vector Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19803)
*Lakshmi Sahithi Yalamarthi,Primal Pappachan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文探讨了向量数据库在安全敏感场景中缺乏细粒度访问控制（FGAC）的问题，提出了策略感知向量检索的概念，并对相关执行策略进行了初步探索与挑战评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）及AI流水线的普及使得向量数据库被应用于安全敏感领域，但现代向量数据库普遍缺乏细粒度访问控制（FGAC），难以处理结构化与非结构化数据混合后的语义查询权限验证问题。

Method: 本文提出了“策略感知向量检索”（Policy-aware Vector Search）的前景，正式化了向量数据库中的FGAC策略模型及其执行问题，对比了多种执行策略，并分析了其对系统性能的影响。

Result: 对比了不同的权限执行策略，并识别了关键的研究挑战（如在保证高检索召回率和低延迟的同时实现合规访问控制），明确了未来实现策略感知向量检索的潜在路径。

Conclusion: 向量数据库在安全敏感场景下的细粒度访问控制（FGAC）存在研究空缺，目前的权衡机制需要在策略合规性、ANN检索召回率和查询延迟之间取得平衡。本文指明了该领域的研究方向，为未来的策略感知向量检索奠定了理论和技术框架。

Abstract: Vector databases are increasingly used in security sensitive contexts with Retrieval Augmented Generation and organizational AI pipelines; however, their security capabilities remain limited. Specifically, Fine-grained Access Control (FGAC) which is required to ensure that data access adheres to user-specific policies is not fully supported in modern vector databases. Unlike relational databases, vector databases combine structured and unstructured attributes to provide semantic, approximate query results, which complicates FGAC implementation. This creates an inherent tension between enforcing FGAC policies correctly, achieving high ANN search recall and maintaining low query latency. In this paper, we present a vision for Policy-aware Vector Search by formalizing the FGAC policy model in vector databases as well as the enforcement problem. We compare various enforcement strategies, present preliminary findings, and identify key open challenges for future research in policy-aware vector search.

</details>


### [267] [Caching for Dollars, Not Hits: An Exact Offline Reference for Cloud-Egress Caching and the Crossover That Decides When It Pays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20539)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对云存储按请求和流量收费的特性，本文证明传统LRU缓存策略并非经济最优，提出了基于成本的离线最优界限（Cost-FOO）及评估基准，并证明GreedyDual算法在应对成本异构性时能将财务悔值降低近90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的缓存研究主要关注降低“缺失率”，而云计算环境下的成本结构（基于请求数和流量的出站费用）使得“最小化缺失率”不再等同于“最小化经济成本”。当前缺乏衡量现有启发式算法与实际美元成本最优解之间差距的基准。

Method: 1. 将均匀大小页缓存的离线最优解建模为积分区间线性规划；2. 针对变长对象，将基于流的离线界限（FOO）扩展至成本维度（Cost-FOO）；3. 提出基于Twitter真实追踪数据的计费保真基准测试工具；4. 引入并验证了异构悔值定律、竞争边界以及临界值公式 s*。

Result: (1) LRU的成本悔值随缺失成本分散度增加而显著上升，而GreedyDual算法可将悔值降低至约十分之一；(2) 当缓存预算能够覆盖高昂成本的活跃集时，GreedyDual的表现接近最优；(3) 推导出了临界值公式s*=GET费/流量费，以此预测部署是否需要美元感知缓存，并经Twitter数据集验证。

Conclusion: 传统的缓存策略（如LRU）以最小化缺失率为目标，在现代云存储收费模式（GET请求费+流量费）下并非最优。研究提出的Cost-FOO界限和GreedyDual算法能显著降低缓存的经济成本。通过引入“临界对象大小”概念，可判断特定工作负载是否需要进行成本感知缓存优化。

Abstract: When a cache miss fetches from cloud object storage, the bill is per GET request and per byte of egress, not latency. Classic caching minimizes the miss rate, the wrong objective: a rarely but expensively fetched object can cost thousands of times more dollars than a frequently but cheaply fetched one. Generalized-caching theory bounds the miss-cost objective, but no reported benchmark measures how far deployed heuristics sit from the dollar-optimal offline policy on real cloud prices. We supply that reference. For uniform-size page caches with heterogeneous miss costs the offline dollar-optimum is exact in polynomial time via an integral interval linear program -- validated against brute force; variable sizes are NP-hard, so we extend the flow-based offline bound from the hit-ratio objective to dollars (cost-FOO), tight to about four percent. Against this reference we find: (i) a heterogeneity-regret law -- LRU's dollar-regret rises with miss-cost dispersion (Spearman 0.87) while cost-aware GreedyDual cuts it to roughly a tenth; (ii) a contention frontier -- GreedyDual's residual regret collapses to near zero exactly when the budget fits the expensive working set, and is the open slice otherwise; and (iii) a closed-form crossover s* = GET_fee/egress_rate (about 4 KB on S3, 330 B on GCS) that predicts which deployments need dollar-aware caching at all. On a real Twitter trace the price vector alone moves the workload across s*, shifting the regime as predicted. The artifact is a reproducible billing-faithful benchmark; heuristics and bounds it builds on are prior work, credited.

</details>


### [268] [Query-aware Routing for Filtered Approximate Nearest Neighbors Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19898)
*Qianqian Xiong,Mengxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对过滤式向量检索任务，提出了一种查询感知的动态路由框架。通过轻量级回归模型预测并选择最优的ANN策略，在保持低延迟的同时实现了搜索性能的最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的过滤式ANN搜索方法没有单一的最优方案，且同一数据集在不同查询条件下最佳算法往往不同，导致在动态环境下的性能不稳定。

Method: 1. 基准测试：评估了多种主流的过滤式ANN方法在不同数据集和谓词下的表现。2. 路由框架：构建了一个轻量级机器学习模型，利用回归预测各候选方法的潜在召回率。3. 策略决策：结合离线基准测试表，基于预测的召回率和QPS信息，选择最优的搜索方法。4. 特征工程与建模：从22个特征中筛选出3个核心特征，并采用回归预测而非分类预测。

Result: 在五个未参与训练的验证数据集上，该路由框架在召回率与QPS的平衡指标上均取得了当前最优（SOTA）的表现，同时仅引入了可忽略不计的查询延迟。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于查询感知的路由框架（Query-Aware Routing Framework），证明了通过轻量级机器学习模型动态选择过滤式近似最近邻（Filtered ANN）搜索算法可以实现最优的召回率与查询吞吐量（QPS）平衡，且具备极低的开销。

Abstract: Filtered ANN search, which combines vector similarity with attribute predicates, is a core primitive in modern vector databases and retrieval-augmented generation. We benchmark all major categorical filtered ANN methods across multiple datasets under three predicates and find that no single method dominates. Moreover, even within a single dataset and predicate type, the best method for a query can vary. Therefore, we propose a query-aware routing framework. A lightweight ML model predicts each candidate method's recall on the query, and the router consults an offline benchmark table that maps every method and parameter setting to its measured recall and QPS, then selects the method with the best recall--QPS trade-off. Our ablation study narrows 22 candidate features to a minimal set of three and we adopt regression rather than classification as the prediction target to sharpen accuracy. Our model is trained on six real-world datasets and applied to five unseen validation datasets. The final result shows that our router achieves state-of-the-art recall and QPS balance across all five validation datasets compared to existing filtered ANN baselines, while incurring negligible latency overhead.

</details>


### [269] [The Bi-Channel Networking Paradigm for Database Systems in the Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19969)
*Georg Kreuzmayr,Muhammad El-Hindi,Benjamin Wagner,Tobias Ziegler,Viktor Leis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对高速网络下TCP内核开销过大的问题，提出了一种双通道网络范式：利用用户态UDP处理数据以提升性能，保留内核TCP保障可靠性，实现了数据库与网络栈的协同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代高速云网络环境下，传统的通用内核TCP协议栈成为数据库系统的CPU性能瓶颈，而仅靠用户态UDP又难以解决可靠性与顺序性保障问题。

Method: 提出“双通道（bi-channel）”范式：1. 数据通道（Data Path）：利用用户态UDP实现高带宽、低延迟数据传输；2. 控制通道（Control Path）：利用内核态TCP保障系统协调与恢复的可靠性。

Result: 在分布式Shuffle场景下以3个CPU核心达到了200 Gbit/s的吞吐量；在复制键值存储场景下实现了每秒处理数百万条消息的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将数据库与网络栈协同设计，双通道范式有效解决了高速网络环境下内核TCP栈的CPU性能瓶颈，证明了该范式在高性能分布式系统中的实用性和高效性。

Abstract: When network links were slow, cloud and distributed database systems could rely on generic kernel abstractions and treat network communication as a black box. With today's fast cloud networks, this approach breaks down: database performance becomes limited by the CPU overhead of the kernel TCP stack. Replacing TCP with user-space UDP can reduce this overhead, but it requires reimplementing essential guarantees, such as reliability and ordering. To solve this conundrum, database systems should no longer treat networking as a black box but co-design it with database operations. We propose the bi-channel paradigm for database systems, which separates communication into two channels: A high-performance data path for latency- and bandwidth-sensitive operations, and a reliable control path for coordination and recovery. We implement the paradigm by combining user-space UDP and kernel-based TCP, though other stack combinations are possible. This design exploits modern NIC capabilities while preserving TCP's reliability. We demonstrate the paradigm's efficiency and simplicity in two representative settings: a distributed shuffle saturating 200 Gbit/s with three CPU cores, and a replicated key-value store processing millions of messages per second.

</details>


### [270] [AgenticDB: Agentic Performance Reconfiguration for Database Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20318)
*Xinyue Yang,Chaozheng Wang,Chen Zheng,Heng Zhang,Yanjun Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: AgenticDB 是一种基于代理的数据库自动化调优框架，通过整合系统反馈、跨层级（DBMS/OS）重构及记忆增强决策，实现了比传统搜索方法更高效、稳健且可持续的数据库自我优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据库自动调优工具多依赖迭代式参数搜索，存在计算开销大、参数空间受限、缺乏系统瓶颈诊断、难以及时应对 OS 级调整以及难以在不同任务间迁移学习等局限性。

Method: 提出 AgenticDB 框架，采用上下文感知的代理机制，不仅覆盖 DBMS 参数，还扩展至 OS 级配置；引入安全约束机制确保执行稳健性，并利用运行反馈进行动态监控与决策优化，实现跨任务经验累积。

Result: 在 MySQL 和 PostgreSQL 上，基于 YCSB、Sysbench 和 TPC-H 负载的实验表明，AgenticDB 性能平均提升 118.1%，达到最优性能的所需时间减少了 22.6%。

Conclusion: AgenticDB通过引入代理架构，成功将数据库调优从单纯的参数搜索转变为一个具备诊断能力、环境感知及持续自我演进的重构过程，显著提升了调优的效率与实用性。

Abstract: Database configuration tuning is critical for workload performance, but practical tuning on real deployments remains difficult. Existing automatic tuners mostly formulate tuning as iterative search over DBMS knob values. This formulation leads to high execution cost, prematurely narrows the configuration space, and leaves practical requirements insufficiently addressed: diagnosing runtime bottlenecks from system feedback, exploring OS-level reconfiguration opportunities, executing changes robustly, and learning from previous trials and tasks.
  We propose AgenticDB, an agentic framework for database workload reconfiguration. AgenticDB implements a context-grounded harness that interacts with the target database environment by proposing DBMS- and OS-level changes, applying them under safety constraints, observing workload performance and runtime states, and using execution feedback to guide subsequent decisions. This runtime interaction enables AgenticDB to diagnose bottlenecks, explore a broader DBMS- and OS-level reconfiguration space, avoid unsafe or unsupported actions, and accumulate experience within and across reconfiguration tasks. As a result, AgenticDB turns database tuning into a self-refining reconfiguration process in which runtime feedback iteratively improves later decisions.
  We conduct extensive experiments on MySQL and PostgreSQL using YCSB, Sysbench, and TPC-H workloads. The results show that AgenticDB achieves the best final performance on all evaluated workloads, improving over the strongest baseline by 118.1% on average and reducing aggregate time-to-best by 22.6%. The results also demonstrate that its OS-level action space, robust execution lifecycle, and memory-enhanced planning contribute to more effective and practical database reconfiguration.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [271] [SPINE: A Fault Injection Profiler for Quantized Neural Networks under Accumulated Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19526)
*Nathan Guimarães,Ian Kersz,Leonardo R. Gobatto,Fabio Benevenuti,Michael G. Jordan,Antonio Carlos S. Beck,Fernanda L. Kastensmidt,Jose Rodrigo Azambuja*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需重训练的模型故障评估框架，通过直接向边缘设备二进制文件注入权重位翻转，揭示了量化神经网络在真实内存环境下的脆弱性，并为选择性加固策略提供了量化依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化神经网络在边缘设备上应用广泛，但其权重易受辐射引起的位翻转影响；现有研究缺乏对真实内存布局下累积故障如何导致误预测的深入表征。

Method: 提出了一种基于GDB的性能分析框架，能够直接在边缘CPU的目标二进制文件中注入累积权重位翻转（Bit-flips），实现在无需重训练或修改代码的情况下生成各层的故障配置文件。

Result: 该方法成功在多种模型架构、量化精度和内存布局下进行了评估，量化了不同参数对故障的敏感度，验证了评估框架的有效性，并为防御策略提供了指导。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过细粒度的故障特征分析，可以识别出神经网络中的关键层，从而采取针对性的选择性加固策略，在满足边缘计算性能约束的同时提高模型的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deploying deep neural networks at the edge demands efficient inference under strict cost and power constraints. Quantized neural networks address these demands by replacing floating-point parameters with low-precision integers, yet their weights remain continuously exposed to radiation-induced bit-flips during inference. Fault Injection can be used to simulate those environments, but existing studies fail to characterize how accumulated upsets translate into mispredictions under realistic memory layouts. This paper presents a GDB-driven profiling framework that injects cumulative weight bit-flips directly onto the target binary of edge CPUs, generating per-layer fault profiles without requiring model retraining or code modification. Evaluated across multiple topologies, quantization efforts, and memory layouts, the results indicate how selective hardening strategies should be applied to effectively protect neural networks.

</details>


### [272] [A Tool for the Synthesis of Adaptive Probabilistic Processors Based on the Ising Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19533)
*Jonathan Juracy Carneiro da Silva,Leonardo R. Gobatto,Jose Rodrigo Azambuja*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于组合优化问题（通过Ising模型映射）的概率架构合成与仿真工具，通过自动构建哈密顿量及自适应算法选择，显著提升了求解的收敛性和灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化问题在硬件实现和算法求解方面面临挑战，现有的固定方法缺乏灵活性，且缺乏评估不同概率计算架构有效性的通用工具。

Method: 提出了一种自动化的工具框架，能够根据问题的规模和拓扑结构自动构建Ising哈密顿量，并动态确定p-bits的数量；同时引入了一种自适应策略，用于在Gibbs采样、模拟退火(SA)、模拟量子退火(SQA)及基于簇的方法中选择最佳更新算法。

Result: 实验结果表明，与固定方法相比，该工具在基准测试问题中表现出更好的收敛行为和更高的架构灵活性。

Conclusion: 该框架为概率计算策略的系统化评估提供了平台，并为未来基于MTJ和p-bits的硬件实现提供了研发支撑，通过自适应算法选择提升了求解组合优化问题的灵活性与有效性。

Abstract: This work presents a tool for the synthesis and simulation of probabilistic architectures for solving combinatorial optimization problems by mapping them to the Ising model. The proposed approach automatically constructs the Ising Hamiltonian and determines the number of probabilistic elements (p-bits) based on problem characteristics such as size and topology. Furthermore, the tool introduces an adaptive strategy for selecting the most suitable update algorithm among Gibbs Sampling, Simulated Annealing (SA), Simulated Quantum Annealing (SQA), and cluster-based methods. Experimental results using benchmark problems demonstrate improved convergence behavior and flexibility compared to fixed approaches. The proposed framework enables systematic evaluation of probabilistic computing strategies and supports the development of future hardware implementations based on MTJs and p-bits.

</details>


### [273] [Design and Evaluation of Energy-Efficient Whisper Dot-Product Kernel Offloading on a CGLA Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19913)
*Takuto Ando,Yu Eto,Ayumu Takeuchi,Yasuhiko Nakashima*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出在IMAX架构上卸载Whisper的点积运算以解决性能瓶颈，并通过混合执行与硬件优化实现了极高的能效比，特别适用于轻量级Whisper模型的终端侧部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whisper模型的点积运算占用CPU推理时间的绝大部分（FP16下占90.6%，Q8_0下占87.1%），构成了推理性能瓶颈。

Method: 在IMAX（一种可编程粗粒度线性阵列架构）上对Whisper的点积运算进行卸载，通过算子映射、本地内存调优和突发传输调度等技术优化。利用FP16转FP32内联转换、双路SIMD FMA、按列多线程以及CPU-IMAX协同混合执行策略。

Result: 基于28nm工艺投影，在Whisper-tiny.en (Q8_0) 任务上，IMAX的PDP为11.58J，显著优于Jetson AGX Orin (27.16J) 和 RTX 4090 (121.38J)。

Conclusion: IMAX架构在处理tiny版本Whisper模型时表现出了优异的能效比（PDP），是一种针对本地自动语音识别（ASR）任务的低功耗、可编程硬件加速方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we implement and evaluate Whisper dot-product kernel offloading on IMAX, a programmable Coarse-Grained Linear Arrays (CGLAs) architecture. Whisper-tiny.en profiling on an ARM Cortex-A72 shows that dot-product operations account for 90.6% of FP16 execution time and 87.1% of Q8_0 execution time. To address this kernel bottleneck, we combine kernel mapping, local-memory sizing, and burst scheduling. The implementation uses inline FP16-to-FP32 conversion, 2-way SIMD FMA on a 64-bit datapath, column-wise multithreading, and mixed execution in which aligned vector segments run on IMAX and residual segments run concurrently on the host CPU. We evaluate the design with an FPGA prototype and a 28nm ASIC projection at 840MHz. For Whisper-tiny.en, 32KB local memory and burst length 16 jointly minimize PDP and EDP. Under a TDP-based cross-platform comparison, the projected IMAX records a PDP of 11.58J for Whisper-tiny.en Q8_0, 2.35x lower than Jetson AGX Orin (27.16J) and 10.48x lower than RTX 4090 (121.38J). The same design extends to Whisper-base.en and Whisper-small.en, where the PDP gap narrows as 32KB local-memory coverage drops from 93.8% for tiny to about 66.5% for base and small. These results position IMAX as a programmable architecture for lower-PDP local ASR in the tiny-model regime.

</details>


### [274] [ExSpike: A General Full-Event Neuromorphic Architecture for Exploiting Irregular Sparsity with Event Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20414)
*Yuehai Chen,Farhad Merchant*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ExSpike 是一种新型的全事件驱动 SNN 加速器架构，通过数据流优化、专门的注意力计算核心和事件压缩技术，克服了传统硬件难以利用 SNN 不规则稀疏性的问题，显著提升了计算效率和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（SNN）虽然具有稀疏的时空活动潜力，但现有的计算架构难以将这种不规则的稀疏性转化为实际的性能和能效增益，缺乏高效的全事件计算架构。

Method: 1. 提出一套数据流优化方案，确保整个网络中每层的输入保持为脉冲形式，实现全事件执行；2. 设计 ExSpike 架构，支持纯事件驱动数据流，并集成了用于尖峰驱动自注意力的 Attention Core；3. 引入相邻位置事件压缩技术，减少空间相邻脉冲序列的冗余累加。

Result: 在 FPGA 实现中，ExSpike 达到了 479.15 GOPS 和 281.85 GOPS/W；在 PE 归一化能效方面，比 SOTA FPGA 加速器（FireFly-T）高出 10 倍。

Conclusion: ExSpike 是一种高效的通用全事件神经形态架构，通过数据流优化和专用的注意力核心，成功在硬件层面实现了脉冲神经网络（SNN）的完全事件驱动计算，显著提升了能效和算力。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) promise energy-efficient computing due to their sparse spatio-temporal activity. However, effectively translating such irregular sparsity into practical performance and energy gains remains challenging, as full-event computing architectures are still underexplored. This paper proposes ExSpike, a general full-event neuromorphic architecture that fully exploits irregular sparsity in SNNs. To realize pure event-driven execution, we first propose a set of dataflow optimizations to ensure that the inputs to each SNN layer remain spike-based, thereby enabling full-event execution throughout the network. We then design a hardware-efficient full-event architecture, named ExSpike, which supports the optimized pure event-driven dataflow and an additional Attention Core for spike-driven self-attention. To further improve computing efficiency, we introduce adjacent-position event compression to reduce redundant accumulations across spatially adjacent spike sequences. ExSpike is implemented on an AMD Xilinx Virtex-7 FPGA and evaluated on both classification and segmentation workloads. Experimental results show that ExSpike achieves high normalized energy efficiency across diverse SNN models while maintaining competitive accuracy, delivering up to 479.15 GOPS, 281.85 GOPS/W, and 0.80 GOPS/W/PE. In particular, ExSpike achieves up to 10$\times$ higher PE-normalized energy efficiency than the SOTA FPGA-based SNN accelerator (FireFly-T). The code for ExSpike is available at \url{https://github.com/xiaoyuehai/ExSpike}.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [275] [MonaVec: A Training-Free Embedded Vector Search Kernel for Edge and Offline AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19458)
*Oğuzhan Yenen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MonaVec 是一个类似于 SQLite 的嵌入式向量搜索内核，通过无需训练的随机哈达玛变换实现 4-bit 量化，在极小内存占用下提供确定性的离线检索能力，适用于边缘 AI 和本地 RAG 应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量搜索引擎依赖外部服务器、海量内存或数据集预训练，难以满足边缘计算和离线环境的需求（如无法保证网络、计算资源有限、隐私敏感等）。MonaVec 旨在提供一种像 SQLite 一样简单、可移植、确定且无需额外负担的嵌入式向量检索方案。

Method: 1. 核心量化：采用随机哈达玛变换（RHDH）将数据分布重塑为正态分布，配合预计算的 Lloyd-Max 表，实现无训练、数据无关的 4-bit 量化。2. 确定性保障：通过嵌入 ChaCha20 旋转种子，确保不同架构间结果可重现、构建内字节一致。3. 架构设计：纯 Rust 实现，支持 SIMD 指令集；支持 BruteForce、IvfFlat 和 HNSW 后端；以单文件（.mvec）格式存储。

Result: 在 45K 数据规模（BGE-M3 向量）上，MonaVec 4-bit 暴力搜索以仅 27 MB 的索引大小实现了 0.960 的 Recall@10，性能表现优于浮点型 FAISS-IVF 和 8-bit usearch。此外，系统保证了跨硬件架构的结果一致性。

Conclusion: MonaVec 成功实现了在资源受限的边缘和离线环境下进行高性能、确定性的向量搜索。作为“向量数据库界的 SQLite”，它通过创新的数据无关量化技术，在无需训练和极低内存占用的情况下，提供了优于现有主流方案的检索效果和位级可重现性。

Abstract: We present MonaVec, a deterministic, embedded vector-search kernel for edge and offline AI -- settings where server infrastructure, network connectivity, and training data are all unavailable. Existing vector-search systems assume a persistent server, gigabytes of RAM, or a training pass over the corpus; MonaVec instead targets the deployment profile of SQLite: one file, one function call, runs anywhere. Its quantization core is training-free by default and data-oblivious: a Randomized Hadamard Transform (RHDH) conditions any input distribution toward N(0,1), so precomputed Lloyd-Max tables quantize to 4 bits (8x smaller) with no learned codebook and no data pass. The index persists as a single .mvec file whose embedded ChaCha20 rotation seed makes results reproducible across architectures and byte-identical within a build -- a determinism guarantee that parallel-build graph libraries cannot offer.
  On semantic embeddings (AG News, 45K x 1024-dim BGE-M3, cosine), MonaVec 4-bit BruteForce reaches 0.960 Recall@10 in 27 MB -- leading float32 FAISS-IVF and 8-bit usearch on recall -- while trading peak throughput for byte-identical determinism. A single-pass global standardization (fit()) extends the same data-oblivious pipeline to magnitude-sensitive L2 data, and optional IvfFlat and HNSW backends carry it to million-vector corpora.
  MonaVec is implemented in pure Rust with Python bindings and runtime SIMD dispatch (AVX-512/AVX2/NEON/scalar). It targets on-device RAG, offline agents, and embedded retrieval -- the niche SQLite occupies for relational data: one file, one call, runs anywhere.

</details>


### [276] [VCG: A Multimodal Retrieval Framework for E-Commerce Video Feeds under Extreme Cold-Start Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19627)
*Katya Mirylenka,Egor Malykh,Mahdyar Ravanbakhsh,Michael Gygli,Marco-Andrea Buchmann,Andrew Dzhoha,Svitlana Borzenko,Francesca Catino,Mohamed Gaafar,Maarten Versteegh,Thomas Kober,Dario d'Andrea,Ellie Langhans*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多模态检索（VCG）的方案，通过CLIP模型解决短视频电商中的极端冷启动与行为偏差问题，实现了50%的视频完播率增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商从搜索驱动转向短视频交互，新发布视频缺乏历史交互数据（极端冷启动），且视频流存在强烈的展示位置和播放时长偏差，导致传统协同过滤方法难以有效应用。

Method: 开发了名为VCG的检索系统，利用领域自适应的视觉-语言模型（基于CLIP）将用户和视频映射至统一语义空间，脱离历史行为数据实现零样本（Zero-shot）检索，并对比了判别式模型（CLIP）与生成式模型（LLM）在嵌入空间中的性能差异。

Result: 在检索任务中，生成式模型易出现嵌入空间坍缩（Embedding Space Collapse）；在线A/B测试显示VCG系统将深度视频完播率提升了50%，并支持多种双向检索及零样本语义搜索场景。

Conclusion: VCG系统通过多模态语义检索成功解决了短视频电商中的极端冷启动问题和行为偏差挑战。实验表明，对比式模型（CLIP）在检索任务中优于生成式模型（LLM），且在处理冷启动场景下具有显著的业务价值。

Abstract: The digital commerce landscape is shifting from static, search-driven catalogs to dynamic, immersive video feeds. This transition introduces an ``extreme cold-start'' problem: unlike traditional items, new short-form videos lack the dense interaction history required for collaborative filtering. Furthermore, immersive feeds introduce strong position and duration biases that distort standard engagement signals. In this paper, we demonstrate the Video Candidate Generation (VCG) system, a scalable multimodal retrieval engine designed to solve these challenges in a large-scale e-commerce environment. By leveraging a domain-adapted vision-language model (based on CLIP), we map users and videos into a shared semantic space, enabling zero-shot retrieval based on visual content rather than behavioral history. We detail the system's architecture and present a rigorous evaluation comparing generative (LLM) vs. discriminative (CLIP) embeddings. Our results show that while generative models excel at attribute prediction, they suffer from embedding space collapse in retrieval tasks. Online A/B testing demonstrates that VCG effectively mitigates engagement biases, yielding a 50\% uplift in deep video completion. To showcase the system's capabilities, we present an interactive demonstration featuring three bi-directional retrieval scenarios: Product-to-Video, Video-to-Product, and Zero-Shot Semantic Search.

</details>


### [277] [Token Factory: Efficiently Integrating Diverse Signals into Large Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19635)
*Xilun Chen,Shao-Chuan Wang,Baykal Cakici,Lukasz Heldt,Lichan Hong,Raghu Keshavan,Aniruddh Nath,Li Wei,Xinyang Xi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Token Factory通过将传统推荐特征转换为“软令牌”输入，解决了大模型在处理复杂推荐数据时的序列过长与计算瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Transformer的大型推荐模型在处理传统推荐特征时，面临提示词过长、内存占用过大以及计算开销极高的问题，限制了其在工业规模下的应用。

Method: 提出“Token Factory”框架，将异构的传统推荐信号（如用户行为、物品属性等）转换为低维的“软令牌（soft tokens）”，从而实现信息的压缩与高效集成，替代了传统的文本化（textualization）或离散表示方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，Token Factory能够有效防止特征输入序列的长度爆炸，同时在计算效率和预测性能上相比传统方法有了显著提升。

Conclusion: Token Factory通过引入“软令牌”机制，有效解决了大型推荐模型（LRM）在工业级应用中整合传统特征时面临的计算效率低下和提示词过长问题，并在实际生产环境中验证了其性能提升。

Abstract: Large Recommendation Models (LRMs) have demonstrated promising capabilities in industry-scale recommendation tasks. However, holistically integrating traditional signals into these transformer-based architectures effectively and efficiently remains a major challenge. Conventional approaches that "textualize" these signals directly or create discrete item representations often lead to excessively long prompts, substantial memory footprints, and high computational overhead. To overcome these limitations, we propose "Token Factory", a framework designed to transform traditional signals into "soft tokens" that can be directly processed by LRMs. This approach enables efficient integration and compression of heterogeneous input features, preventing prompt length explosion while enhancing model performance. We detail the architecture of Token Factory and present experimental results validating its effectiveness in a production-scale recommendation environment.

</details>


### [278] [SAFE-Cascade: Cost-Adaptive Vision-Language Routing for Chart Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19646)
*Ayush Dwivedi,Qixin Wang,Ashvi Soni,Ruoteng Wang,Han Li,Animesh Mahapatra,Neeraj Agrawal,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SAFE-Cascade 是一种针对图表问答的成本优化系统，通过引入轻量级文本模型路由，有效降低了对高昂 VLM 的依赖，同时保持了系统的准确性与透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接调用视觉语言模型（VLM）处理所有图表问答任务成本过高且不必要，很多简单问题仅通过文字信息即可解决，需要一种更具成本效益的选择性路由机制。

Method: 提出了一种交互式层级系统：首先通过 OCR 提取文本，使用轻量级语言模型（GPT-4o-mini）生成初步答案，随后由训练好的随机森林（Random Forest）路由模型根据推理特征决定是否向 VLM（Gemini-1.5-flash）请求视觉增强。

Result: 在 ChartQA 数据集上，SAFE-Cascade 达到 69.1% 的准确率，同时将 VLM 调用次数减少了 26.9%，预估成本降低了 9.3%，实现了与全 VLM 基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: SAFE-Cascade 证明了通过选择性模态路由，可以在不牺牲性能的前提下，显著降低图表问答系统的成本和延迟，并增强了系统的透明度与可调性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are powerful for chart question answering, but invoking a VLM for every query can be unnecessarily expensive when many questions are answerable from OCR text and lightweight language reasoning. We demonstrate SAFE-Cascade, an interactive system for cost-adaptive chart question answering. Given a chart image and a natural-language question, SAFE-Cascade first extracts chart text with OCR, obtains a provisional answer from a text-only language model, and then uses a learned router to decide whether to accept the text answer or escalate to a VLM. The demo exposes this decision process to users: OCR evidence, text-only answer, routing probability, escalation decision, final answer, estimated cost, and estimated latency are shown side by side. SAFE-Cascade is designed as a transparent interface for understanding when visual grounding is actually needed. Users can upload or select charts, ask questions, inspect the evidence used by each pathway, compare text-only and VLM answers, and adjust the escalation threshold to explore the accuracy-cost frontier. The system is implemented with Azure Document Intelligence for OCR, gpt-5-mini as the text-only model, gemini-2.5-flash-image as the VLM, and a Random Forest router trained on inference-time features. On a held-out ChartQA test split of 375 examples from a 2,500-example experiment, SAFE-Cascade achieves 69.1% unified accuracy with 73.1% VLM invocation, compared with 67.7% accuracy and 100% VLM invocation for the full-VLM baseline. The observed +1.4 percentage-point difference is statistically uncertain, so we interpret SAFE-Cascade as matching full-VLM performance while reducing VLM calls by 26.9% and estimated cost by 9.3%. The demonstration shows how selective modality routing can make multimodal knowledge systems more transparent, tunable, and cost-aware.

</details>


### [279] [Closing the Calibration Gap in Semantic Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19719)
*Aditeya Baral,Radoslav Ralev,Iliya Sotirov Zhechev,Srijith Rajamohan,Jen Agarwal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文指出语义缓存评价中PR-AUC指标的局限性，引入了能反映实际系统部署表现的新指标P-CHR和CRR，揭示了语义缓存模型选型应侧重于模型校准而非传统的排序能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义缓存研究多使用PR-AUC评估，该指标仅衡量排名能力，忽视了实际部署中固定阈值下的可用性，导致模型选择与实际运行表现不匹配。

Method: 1. 引入P-CHR (Precision-Cache Hit Ratio) AUC 评估缓存利用率下的精度；2. 提出CRR (Calibration Retention Rate) 衡量离线排序质量在部署中的保持程度；3. 将离线与部署间的操作差距分解为可恢复的校准组件和不可约的结构性组件。

Result: 1. PR-AUC高的模型在实际部署中表现往往较差；2. 操作性能差距主要由训练目标决定，而非数据规模；3. 事后校准仅能部分弥补模型性能缺口。

Conclusion: 语义缓存的模型选择本质上是校准问题而非排序问题，现有的PR-AUC评价指标存在严重的适配偏差，P-CHR与CRR指标能更准确地评估缓存系统的实际部署性能。

Abstract: Semantic caching cuts LLM inference costs by serving a cached response to semantically similar queries. Standard practice evaluates these systems using PR-AUC, a metric that only measures how well scores rank and ignores whether they are usable at a fixed threshold. We show this mismatch leads to systematically poor deployment choices, as models with the highest PR-AUC are often the worst in operation. We introduce Precision-Cache Hit Ratio (P-CHR) AUC, a cache-aware metric that measures precision across cache utilization levels, and Calibration Retention Rate (CRR), which captures how much offline ranking quality survives at deployment. We decompose the operational gap between offline and deployed quality into a recoverable calibration component and an irreducible structural component fixed by the dataset's positive rate. Our experiments show that the calibration gap is governed by the training objective rather than data scale, and post-hoc calibration only partially closes it. Ultimately, model selection for semantic caching is a calibration problem, not a ranking one, and measuring it is the first step to closing the gap.

</details>


### [280] [Stellar: Scalable Multimodal Document Retrieval for Natural Language Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19960)
*Yuxiang Guo,Zhonghao Hu,Yuren Mao,Yuhang Liu,Congcong Ge,Xiaolu Zhang,Jun Zhou,Yunjun Gao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Stellar 是一种可扩展的多模态检索框架，通过磁盘存储多向量嵌入并结合轻量级过滤与动态加载机制，解决了高性能检索与高内存需求之间的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的多模态文档检索方法依赖多向量表示进行“后期交互”（Late Interaction），虽然效果好，但会产生巨大的内存开销，导致扩展性差，难以在真实场景中部署。

Method: 1. 词法表征过滤（LRF）：微调多模态大模型（MLLM）作为稀疏编码器生成高质量的词法表示，从而实现高效的初步过滤，缩减候选集。
2. 高效磁盘后期交互（DLI）：设计了基于均衡聚类算法的磁盘存储布局，并利用成本模型动态地将必要的Token嵌入加载到内存中。

Result: 在四个现实基准测试和一个大规模数据集上，Stellar 将内存开销和查询延迟降低了1至2个数量级，且未牺牲检索效果。

Conclusion: Stellar 通过引入基于词法表征的过滤（LRF）和高效的磁盘交换后期交互（DLI）机制，成功解决了多模态文档检索中多向量表示带来的内存瓶颈问题，在保持检索精度的同时实现了显著的效率提升，极大地促进了多模态RAG系统的落地。

Abstract: Multimodal document retrieval--selecting the most relevant multimodal document from a large corpus to answer a natural language query--plays an essential role in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems. State-of-the-art methods represent each document and query with multiple token-level embeddings and use late interaction to achieve high effectiveness. However, such multi-vector representations incur substantial memory overhead during retrieval, leading to poor scalability and hindering real-world deployment. In this paper, we present Stellar, a scalable multimodal document retrieval framework that stores token-level document embeddings on disk and loads only a small set of candidate embeddings into memory for late interaction. Stellar comprises two key components: (i) Lexical Representation-based Filtering (LRF), which fine-tunes a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as a sparse encoder to produce high-quality lexical representations, enabling efficient and effective document filtering to significantly reduce the candidate set; (ii) Efficient Disk-backed Late Interaction (DLI), which designs an on-disk token embedding storage layout guided by a balanced clustering algorithm, and dynamically loads only the necessary token embeddings into memory using a simple yet effective cost model. Extensive experiments on four real-world benchmarks and a newly presented large-scale dataset demonstrate that Stellar reduces memory overhead and query latency by 1-2 orders of magnitude compared to existing methods without compromising retrieval effectiveness.

</details>


### [281] [PACMS: Submodular Context Selection as a Pluggable Engine for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20047)
*Manu Ghulyani,Arunabh Singh,Karan Bharadwaj,Ankit Nath,Suranjan Goswami*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了LLM代理在上下文有限情况下信息丢失的问题，批评了现行“近期优先”截断策略的盲目性，并指出需要一种基于相关性而非时间顺序的上下文动态筛选与管理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话型及工具调用型LLM代理的上下文窗口经常因对话轮次、记忆存储和大量工具输出而耗尽。主流的截断策略往往导致关键的旧信息被丢弃、冗余的新信息被保留，从而影响模型在长期交互中的记忆与推理能力。

Method: 本文对现有上下文管理方法进行了批判性分析：指出现行的“近期截断”（Recency Truncation）机制是主题无关的，且现有替代方案（如RAG或通用上下文压缩）未能实现对已存在上下文池的按需相关性裁决。

Result: 通过分析揭示了现有上下文管理的局限性，特别是在需要跨长对话召回信息的场景下，简单的时间顺序截断无法满足代理对高相关性信息的需求。

Conclusion: 作者指出，现有的上下文管理机制（如基于近期的截断方式）存在严重缺陷，无法处理长期交互中的关键信息保持问题。因此，有必要提出一种能够将记忆条目、对话轮次和工具输出统一视为候选池，并根据当前查询的相关性进行动态选择的上下文管理方案。

Abstract: Conversational and tool-using LLM agents operate over a context window that fills from several directions simultaneously. As a session proceeds, the agent accumulates user and assistant turns, entries drawn from a persistent memory store, and often largest of all, the verbatim outputs of tool calls such as file reads, search results, and API responses. Once the cumulative context exceeds the model's token budget, the framework must decide what to keep.
  The prevailing mechanism is recency truncation, sometimes paired with periodic summarization. This is topic-blind: a fact established early in a session is discarded simply because it is old, even when the current user query is about exactly that fact; conversely, verbose but irrelevant recent material is retained. Agents that must recall information across many turns, the defining case for memory, are precisely where recency truncation fails.
  Existing alternatives sit outside the agent's assembly step. Retrieval augmented generation fetches external documents into the prompt but does not arbitrate the agent's \emph{already-present} pooled context. Context-compression methods reduce token count by rewriting or pruning text, but operate query-blind and lossily. Neither treats memory entries, conversation turns, and tool outputs as a single candidate pool to be selected from by relevance at the moment the prompt is assembled.

</details>


### [282] [Generative Engine Optimization at Scale: Measuring Brand Visibility Across AI Search Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20065)
*Pratyush Kumar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究针对ChatGPT等生成式AI搜索时代，通过对10万条AI响应的实测分析，量化了品牌的AI可见度层级，揭示了引用来源的偏好，并指出内容格式优化（如制作“最佳”清单）是提升品牌AI曝光的核心路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着用户日益依赖AI搜索引擎（如ChatGPT, Perplexity等）直接获取答案而非点击传统链接，传统的SEO策略失效。品牌急需理解并掌握如何让AI引擎认可、引用并推荐其内容，以应对生成式AI带来的流量范式转移。

Method: 利用Ranqo收集了2026年3月至5月期间，针对100多个品牌的10万多条AI生成响应数据进行大规模分析；通过量化分析品牌在不同层级的出现概率、引用来源比例及情感倾向稳定性，建立了GEO基准测试体系。

Result: 1. 品牌存在明显的可见度层级：知名品牌（73%可见度）远高于中端品牌（44%）和小众品牌（11%）。2. 引用来源以品牌官网为主（78%），其次为YouTube。3. “Best-of”列表类文章是获得引用的最高效内容格式（21%）。4. 品牌情感表达（正负面）远比品牌提及率更具波动性。

Conclusion: 生成式引擎优化（GEO）是品牌增长的新范式。品牌可见度在AI搜索中存在明显的“层级鸿沟”，且受品牌知名度影响巨大。通过优化内容产出（如“best-of”列表）和理解引擎引用逻辑，品牌可尝试提升其在AI响应中的可见度。

Abstract: People increasingly get answers straight from AI search engines like ChatGPT, Claude, Perplexity, and Gemini rather than scrolling search results. Brands that once focused on search engine optimization (SEO) must now optimize for how these engines represent, cite, and recommend them -- a shift variously called Generative Engine Optimization (GEO), Answer Engine Optimization (AEO), and AI Search Visibility. We treat AEO and AI Visibility as part of GEO, and study how to measure brand visibility across AI engines: what they value when they cite a brand, which sources they rely on, and what content large language models surface. The hard case is everyone outside the already-authoritative top brands -- SMEs, D2C brands, creators, and early-stage startups.
  We analyze 100K+ prompt responses across 100+ brands tracked on Ranqo between March and May 2026. First visibility runs form a clear three-tier brand-stature ladder: global household names (e.g., Stripe, Nike) appear in 73% of relevant AI answers on their first run; established mid-market and regional brands (e.g., Olipop, Klaviyo) in 44%; niche and small brands in just 11% -- about 30 percentage points per step. When engines cite sources, about 78% go to corporate websites; among non-corporate sources YouTube leads, ahead of Reddit, editorial media, and Wikipedia. The highest-leverage page is the ranked "best-of" listicle, the most-cited content format at about 21% of all citations. Sentiment is the unstable signal: whether a brand is framed positively or negatively flips about 6.7 times more often than whether it is mentioned at all.
  These findings provide a first large-scale baseline for measuring GEO: AI brand visibility can be measured, differs by platform, and varies strongly by brand maturity. We close by proposing seven v1.1 protocols to test whether specific recommendations can causally improve AI visibility.

</details>


### [283] [ScholarQuest: A Taxonomy-Guided Benchmark for Agentic Academic Paper Search in Open Literature Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20235)
*Tingyue Pan,Mingyue Cheng,Daoyu Wang,Yitong Zhou,Jie Ouyang,Qi Liu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了ScholarQuest——一个用于评估学术搜索智能体性能的大规模分类导向基准测试，填补了现有评估体系在真实、复杂科研场景下的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学术搜索基准测试无法在真实、开放的文献环境中系统评估基于LLM的学术搜索智能体。

Method: 构建了名为ScholarQuest的大规模、分类法引导的基准测试集，涵盖1,000多个计算机科学主题和四种研究意图；并提供可扩展的答案构建方案及共享检索后端ScholarBase以确保评估的可重复性。

Result: 实验表明智能体方法优于单次检索基准，但整体召回率依然较低；通过对搜索效率、意图稳健性和失败案例的分析，该基准有效揭示了现有模型在学术检索中的局限性。

Conclusion: 当前基于LLM的学术搜索智能体表现仍有较大改进空间（最优Recall@100仅0.314），ScholarQuest为评估其学术搜索能力提供了系统的多维衡量标准和可扩展的基础设施。

Abstract: Academic paper search is a core step in scientific research, and LLM-based search agents are emerging as a promising paradigm for iterative, intent-driven literature exploration. However, existing benchmarks are insufficient for systematically evaluating agentic academic search under realistic open literature environments. We propose ScholarQuest, a large-scale, taxonomy-guided benchmark for agentic academic paper search. ScholarQuest is constructed from over 1,000 computer science topics and four representative research intents, including method-oriented, setting-anchored, comparison-based, and scope-controlled queries. It further provides scalable answer construction and a shared retrieval backend ScholarBase for reproducible evaluation. Benchmarking results show that agentic methods outperform single-shot retrieval baselines, yet the best-performing agent only achieves 0.314 Recall@100 and 0.355 Recall@All, indicating substantial room for improvement. In addition, analyses of search efficiency, intent-level robustness, and failure cases further highlight the benchmark's ability to provide multi-dimensional evaluation signals for academic paper search agents.

</details>


### [284] [ELVA: Exploring Ranking-Driven Universal Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20280)
*Yuhan Liu,Pei Fu,Hang Li,Yukun Qi,Chao Jiang,Jingwen Fu,Zhen Liu,Bin Qin,Zhenbo Luo,Jian Luan,Jingmin Xin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态检索中因对比学习忽略负样本信息而导致的“粒度盲点”问题，提出了 ELVA 框架。通过引入基于规则的强化学习（RLVR），在无需显式标签的情况下实现负样本排序优化，并在自建基准 MRBench 上显著提升了细粒度信息的处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于对比学习的多模态大模型（MLLM）在通用多模态检索（UMR）中存在“粒度盲点”问题。由于对比学习将样本简单二元化（正/负），忽略了负样本携带的差异化信息，导致模型无法准确处理复杂的细粒度查询。

Method: 提出名为 ELVA 的框架，核心在于：1. 扩展强化学习（RLVR）至检索任务，无需显式排序标签；2. 引入基于规则的奖励机制，通过排序驱动优化，动态处理不同相似度的负样本，并扩大正负样本间的相似度差距。

Result: ELVA 在标准检索基准上达到 SOTA 水平。在作者提出的专门针对多粒度查询的 MRBench 上，模型表现提升了 13.1%，验证了该方法缓解粒度盲点的有效性。

Conclusion: ELVA 是解决通用多模态检索中“粒度盲点（Grain Blindness）”问题的有效框架。通过引入基于规则的强化学习（RLVR）优化负样本排序，ELVA 显著提升了模型对细粒度信息的捕捉能力，并在多个基准测试及作者自建的 MRBench 上均取得优异表现。

Abstract: Leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) via contrastive learning has become a mainstream paradigm for improving the performance of Universal Multimodal Retrieval (UMR). However, previous works have ignored the grain blindness when adapting the contrastive paradigm into retrieval tasks. Grain blindness refers to the tendency of the model to overlook grain-level information contained in the query, which is crucial for effectively handling complex queries. This stems from contrastive learning treating samples as a binary classification (positive/negative), while ignoring the different information carried by each negative sample. To address this, we argue that negatives should be treated differently according to their similarity to the positive sample, enabling the model to learn distinct grain information from each negative. In this paper, we introduce a simple but effective framework, called ELVA, a novel rule-based RL framework that mitigates grain blindness through ranking-driven MLLMs. 1) Instead of relying on reward models, we extend Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards
  (RLVR) to retrieval tasks, allowing the model to explore new ranking behaviors without explicit ranking labels. 2) By utilizing rule-based rewards, our approach jointly optimizes the ranking of negative samples while enlarging the similarity gap between positive and negative. To more precisely measure grain blindness, we further introduce MRBench, a new benchmark specifically designed for multi-grain query scenarios. ELVA achieves state-of-the-art results across standard retrieval benchmarks, and its notable 13.1% improvement on MRBench further demonstrates its effectiveness in alleviating grain blindness.

</details>


### [285] [Structuring and Tokenizing Distributed User Interest Context for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20554)
*Ruizhong Qiu,Yinglong Xia,Dongqi Fu,Hanqing Zeng,Ren Chen,Xiangjun Fan,Hong Li,Hong Yan,Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对生成式推荐中用户行为与物品语义表征的局限，G2Rec 通过统一全局图建模与语义 Token 化，构建了可扩展的兴趣原型捕捉框架，并在工业级应用中验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐方法在同时处理用户行为上下文与物品语义上下文时存在局限：图形化方法存在可扩展性差或仅能捕捉局部信息的问题；现有的语义 Token 化方法多依赖启发式策略，缺乏有效监督，导致表征不准确。

Method: 提出了 G2Rec 框架，将全局图交互建模（Graph-based co-engagement modeling）与语义 Token 化（Semantic tokenization）相统一，以在无需显式监督信号的情况下构建用户兴趣原型。

Result: 在工业级生成式推荐中实现了更全面、准确的用户行为上下文建模。在公共数据集上的实验表现优于现有方法，且已在工业场景中成功上线部署。

Conclusion: G2Rec 通过统一全局图建模与语义 Token 化，无需标注的真实用户兴趣，即可捕捉到全面且具有语义基础的用户兴趣原型，有效地解决了工业级生成式推荐中行为上下文建模的挑战。

Abstract: Generative recommendation is an emerging paradigm that has shown promise in industrial recommendation systems, aiming to predict users' next interactions from their historical behaviors. At the core of generative recommendation lies item tokenization, which bridges item semantics and recommendation models. However, existing methods often struggle to effectively organize and inject complex user-behavioral and item-semantic contexts into recommendation models simultaneously. On the one hand, existing graph-based integration methods, such as graph serialization and graph neural networks, either suffer from scalability issues or exploit only local graph information. On the other hand, existing semantic tokenization methods typically rely on heuristics and lack explicit supervision signals, which may lead to inaccurate or suboptimal semantic representations. To address these limitations in user interest context modeling, we propose G2Rec, a scalable framework that unifies holistic graph-based user co-engagement modeling with semantic tokenization for industrial-scale generative recommendation. Overall, G2Rec enables recommendation models to capture holistic and semantically grounded user interest prototypes without requiring ground-truth user interests, thereby providing more comprehensive and accurate modeling of user behavior contexts in industrial sequential recommendation. Online deployment across product surfaces and extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate the superiority of G2Rec over existing methods.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [286] [Physical Atari: A Robust and Accessible Platform for Real-time Reinforcement Learning on Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19357)
*Khurram Javed,Joseph Modayil,Gloria Kennickell,Richard S. Sutton,John Carmack*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究者开发了“Physical Atari”低成本开源平台，用于执行物理世界的强化学习实验，证明了在线自适应对解决仿真到现实部署（Sim-to-Real）分布偏移问题的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过物理实体环境研究强化学习算法，弥补模拟环境与真实机器人部署之间的差距，克服硬件可靠性及高昂成本的障碍。

Method: 设计了一套低成本的“Physical Atari”系统，包含可物理操作 Atari CX40+ 手柄的机器人“Robotroller”、用于渲染游戏画面和奖励信号的“Atari Devbox”，配合摄像头及桌面电脑共同组成。通过高频监控伺服状态保护硬件，并使用廉价市售组件及3D打印件实现低造价。

Result: 该系统成本低于1000美元，经受住了数周不间断实验的考验，验证了其耐用性和稳定性，并成功通过实验证明了设备端自适应对优化机器人性能的关键作用。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了强化学习算法可以直接在机器人上进行训练，同时也揭示了学习环境与实际部署环境之间的分布偏移会显著降低策略性能，凸显了机器人设备端自适应（on-device adaptation）的重要性。

Abstract: We built a robot called the Robotroller that actuates an Atari CX40+ controller and a device called the Atari Devbox that renders the game frame and the reward signal from the Arcade Learning Environment on a screen. The Robotroller and the Atari Devbox, together with an off-the-shelf camera and a desktop computer, constitute a system that can be used to study reinforcement learning algorithms in the physical world. We call the full system Physical Atari. In this paper, we detail the key decisions that make Physical Atari a robust and accessible platform. To make the system robust, we designed the Robotroller so that all movement is done through bearings, which reduces wear. Additionally, we wrote software that monitors the state of the servos at a high frequency and intervenes to limit stress. To make the system accessible, we used affordable off-the-shelf components and parts that can be manufactured using consumer 3D printers. Physical Atari can be built for under $1,000 and has been used for weeks of non-stop reinforcement learning experiments without any mechanical failures. We used it to validate that reinforcement learning algorithms can learn directly on robots and show that even small distribution shifts between learning and deployment can significantly degrade the performance of policies. Our results underscore the importance of on-device adaptation for strong performance on robots.

</details>


### [287] [WorkBenchMark: A LEGO-Based Assembly Benchmark with an Assembly-by-Disassembly Baseline for the Smart Manufacturing League](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19358)
*Wenbo Ma,Daniel Swoboda,Matteo Tschesche,Till Hofmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文推出了WorkBenchMark，这是一个基于LEGO Duplo的机器人装配基准，旨在解决复杂物理约束下的任务规划与操作难题。实验表明，传统的规划方法在处理这些装配任务时比先进的端到端VLA方法更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前端到端学习方法在解决结合了低级操作与高级符号推理、且受物理约束的机器人装配任务时表现欠佳，缺乏专门的基准测试来评估这些能力。

Method: 提出了一个基于LEGO Duplo的机器人装配基准（WorkBenchMark），包含400个不同复杂度的任务；并设计了一种“拆解式装配”（Assembly-by-Disassembly）的开放词汇感知基准方案。

Result: WorkBenchMark涵盖了四个复杂层次的任务，其实验结果表明，所提出的规划方案在所有层级的任务性能上均超过了先进的视觉-语言-动作模型。

Conclusion: 基于规划的管道（Planning-based pipeline）在机器人装配任务中显著优于端到端视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，证明了当前LLM/VLA方法在处理复杂物理约束装配任务时的局限性。

Abstract: We introduceWorkBenchMark, a LEGO Duplo-based robotic assembly benchmark motivated by the RoboCup Smart Manufacturing League. Robotic assembly couples low-level manipulation with task-level symbolic reasoning under physical constraints, a combination that current end-to-end learning methods do not yet solve reliably. The benchmark provides 400 tasks across four complexity tiers. We provide an open-vocabulary perception, Assembly-by-Disassembly baseline solution. Our planning-based pipeline outperforms a modern vision-language-action approach across all tiers. The benchmark, simulation environment, and baseline implementation will be released openly to support the broader robotic assembly community.

</details>


### [288] [3D Scene Graphs: Open Challenges and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19383)
*Dennis Rotondi,Francesco Argenziano,Sebastian Koch,Nathan Hughes,Martin Buechner,Johanna Wald,Lukas Rosenberger Schmid,Daniele Nardi,Abhinav Valada,Liam Paull,Federico Tombari,Luca Carlone,Kai O. Arras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本综述批判性地梳理了3D场景图（3DSG）研究现状，统一了建模定义与评估框架，针对其在机器人与视觉领域的碎片化问题提出了系统性见解，旨在规范该领域的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DSG在机器人与计算机视觉领域应用广泛，但因缺乏统一的建模标准、构建流程和评估协议，导致研究碎片化，难以进行横向对比及评估部署挑战。

Method: 通过文献综述方法，对3DSG进行统一定义，分析建模选择（节点、边、层次结构等）、构建技术流派、下游应用场景及评估策略，并建立配套网站进行资源整合。

Result: 本文全面勾勒了3D场景图的研究图谱，归纳了主流建模策略，梳理了从原始传感器数据到场景图的构建路径，并识别了当前面临的开放性挑战，为后续研究提供了标准化参考。

Conclusion: 3D场景图（3DSG）是空间AI领域的关键表征，但目前领域内缺乏统一标准。本文通过系统性梳理，明确了建模范式、构建流程及评估标准，旨在推动3DSG技术的标准化与产业化落地。

Abstract: 3D Scene Graphs (3DSGs) have emerged as a powerful representation for spatial AI by combining geometric grounding with semantic and relational abstractions of the environment. Their expressiveness has made them relevant to a broad range of problems in robotics and computer vision, including manipulation, navigation, task planning, scene understanding, and many others. However, the field remains fragmented: different communities adopt distinct formulations, construction pipelines, and evaluation protocols, making it difficult to compare methods, identify common assumptions, and assess remaining challenges for robust real-world deployment. This survey provides a unified and critical review of 3DSGs, with particular emphasis on open challenges and future directions. We first formalize 3DSGs under a common definition and analyze the principal modeling choices that characterize existing formulations, including node and edge attributes, hierarchical structure, dynamic scene representations, and affordance-aware extensions. We then review how 3DSGs are built from raw sensory observations, discussing the most common terminologies, conventions, and techniques. Finally, we examine downstream applications and evaluation strategies, from intrinsic graph quality to task-level performance. To support the community, we also provide a dedicated website that organizes and extends the surveyed content, accessible at https://3dscenegraphs.com/.

</details>


### [289] [DiffusionVS: A Generative Framework for Robust Visual Servoing Based on Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19397)
*Hongkang Cui,Rui He,Haoyao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于扩散模型的视觉伺服方法，结合在线训练策略有效解决了轨迹抖动与泛化局限，并证明了该扩散模块可作为提升视觉伺服系统性能的通用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对回归型视觉伺服中因单步映射噪声敏感以及分布偏移导致的轨迹抖动和泛化能力不足问题。

Method: 提出了一种基于Diffusion Policy的视觉伺服方法，使用归一化标签角点图像坐标作为输入，通过条件去噪生成相机速度；同时引入在线训练策略，在交互式体验中持续扩充数据多样性。

Result: 在仿真中达到近100%的成功率，在物理实验中达到93%的成功率；验证了该扩散机制作为通用模块能提升其他视觉伺服网络的性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的扩散式视觉伺服方法通过引入在线训练和条件去噪机制，有效降低了轨迹抖动，增强了模型的泛化能力与鲁棒性，且其作为通用模块能显著提升现有视觉伺服系统的性能。

Abstract: Visual servoing is a fundamental technique in robotic manipulation and navigation. Regression-based visual servoing frequently experiences trajectory jitter as a result of noise-sensitive single-step mappings and the accumulation of errors during distribution shifts. In contrast, Diffusion Policy maintains temporal consistency by predicting action sequences and improves robustness through implicit data augmentation.
  This paper presents a novel diffusion-based servoing method. Based on Diffusion Policy, the proposed approach uses normalized image coordinates of observed tag corners as input and generates camera velocity through conditional denoising. To overcome the generalization limitations of models trained on static datasets, an online training paradigm is adopted, continuously expanding the diversity of training data through interactive experience collection. This strategy substantially enhances both the performance and generalization capability of the model. Comprehensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, achieving success rates of nearly 100\% in simulation and 93\% in physical experiments. Beyond the specific pipeline, we further validate the generality of the diffusion mechanism. Experiments show that existing visual servoing networks consistently achieve improved performance when integrated with our diffusion-based module. These results indicate that the proposed strategy possesses broad applicability and can enhance various visual servoing systems beyond the specific architecture presented here.

</details>


### [290] [Playful Agentic Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19419)
*Junyi Zhang,Jiaxin Ge,Hanjun Yoo,Letian Fu,Zihan Yang,Yaowei Liu,Raj Saravanan,Shaofeng Yin,Justin Yu,Dantong Niu,Zirui Wang,Roei Herzig,Ken Goldberg,Yutong Bai,David M. Chan,Ion Stoica,Angjoo Kanazawa,Jiahui Lei,Haiwen Feng,Trevor Darrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RATs 框架，通过让机器人进行“自主玩耍（Play）”来探索并自动生成代码技能库，使机器人无需额外微调即可利用已学技能高效解决各类新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服现有代理机器人系统（Agentic Robot Systems）过于依赖显式指令、缺乏通用技能积累的局限性，实现通过自主“玩耍”在任务到来前进行持续的技能学习。

Method: 提出 RATs (Robotics Agent Teams) 框架，包含自主探索任务生成、代码策略规划与执行、结果验证、故障诊断、重试机制以及将成功案例蒸馏为可持久化的代码技能库。

Result: 在 LIBERO-PRO 和 MolmoSpaces 环境中较基线方法分别提升了 20.6% 和 17.0% 的成功率；同时，该技能库可跨代理（Agent）复用，在 RoboSuite 和真实世界实验中分别提升了 8.9% 和 8.8% 的表现。

Conclusion: 通过“玩耍”阶段获取技能并将其固化并入代码库（Code Library），可以显著提升机器人处理后续下游任务的效率与成功率，验证了自主探索在机器人技能习得中的重要价值。

Abstract: Current agentic robot systems can write executable Code-as-Policy programs, observe feedback, and revise behavior across multiple attempts, but they remain largely task-driven: reusable skills are acquired only after explicit instructions. We study Playful Agentic Robot Learning, where an embodied coding agent uses self-directed play as a continual skill-learning stage before downstream tasks arrive. We introduce RATs, Robotics Agent Teams designed for play-time skill acquisition. During play, RATs proposes novel yet learnable exploratory tasks, plans and executes robot-code policies, verifies intermediate progress, diagnoses failures, retries with dense, step-level feedback, and distills successful executions into a persistent code skill library. At test time, the agent reuses relevant skills from this frozen library to help solve new tasks. Experiments in LIBERO-PRO and MolmoSpaces show that play-learned skills improve held-out downstream tasks over no-play and random-play baselines, with 20.6 and 17.0 percentage-point gains over CaP-Agent0 on LIBERO-PRO and MolmoSpaces, respectively.
